Revit exporter 0.5.3 - source concatenation for Claude Chat
Original files and paths are authoritative. Read CHANGES-0.5.3.md, ACCEPTANCE-TEST.md and CURRENT_CONTRACT.md first.


===== FILE: build/build-exporters.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cross-builds the six Revit exporter variants (needs the .NET 10 SDK and NuGet
# access; no Revit). Output: <out>/<year>-<framework>/Offline3DPrototype.Revit<year>.dll (+ .pdb)
# Usage: build/build-exporters.sh [out-dir]
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
OUT="$(realpath -m "${1:-$ROOT/out/exporters}")"
export DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1 DOTNET_NOLOGO=1
rm -rf "$OUT"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
for variant in 2024:net48 2025:net8.0-windows 2025:net10.0-windows 2026:net8.0-windows 2026:net10.0-windows 2027:net10.0-windows; do
  year=${variant%%:*}; fw=${variant#*:}
  host=""; [ "$fw" != "net48" ] && host="-p:RevitHostFramework=$fw"
  stage="$(mktemp -d)"
  dotnet build "$ROOT/src/RevitExporter/RevitExporter.csproj" -c Release -p:RevitYear=$year $host \
    -p:RestoreLockedMode=false -p:RestorePackagesWithLockFile=false -o "$stage" -v q -nologo > "$OUT/build-$year-$fw.txt" 2>&1 \
    || { cat "$OUT/build-$year-$fw.txt"; exit 1; }
  mkdir -p "$OUT/$year-$fw"
  cp "$stage/Offline3DPrototype.Revit$year.dll" "$stage/Offline3DPrototype.Revit$year.pdb" "$OUT/$year-$fw/"
  for f in RevitAPI.dll RevitAPIUI.dll; do [ ! -e "$OUT/$year-$fw/$f" ] || { echo "Autodesk references must not be redistributed"; exit 1; }; done
  rm -rf "$stage"
  echo "$year $fw: $(grep -E 'Warn|Error' "$OUT/build-$year-$fw.txt" | tr '\n' ' ')"
done
( cd "$OUT" && sha256sum */*.dll > SHA256SUMS.txt )
echo "Exporters in $OUT"


===== FILE: build/package-update.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds the six exporters and assembles the drop-in update ZIP for an existing
# 0.5.2 installation. Usage: build/package-update.sh [out-dir]
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
OUT="$(realpath -m "${1:-$ROOT/out}")"
NAME="Offline3D-RevitExporter-0.5.3-update"
STAGE="$OUT/$NAME"
rm -rf "$STAGE" "$OUT/$NAME.zip"; mkdir -p "$STAGE"
"$ROOT/build/build-exporters.sh" "$OUT/exporters"
mkdir -p "$STAGE/Exporters"
for d in "$OUT/exporters"/*/; do cp -r "$d" "$STAGE/Exporters/"; done
cp "$ROOT/build/update-package/Update-Offline3DExporter.ps1" "$ROOT/build/update-package/README.txt" "$ROOT/ACCEPTANCE-TEST.md" "$STAGE/"
cp "$ROOT/build/update-package/UPDATE.cmd" "$ROOT/build/update-package/ROLLBACK.cmd" "$STAGE/"
# Windows line endings for the launchers and text files.
for f in "$STAGE"/*.cmd "$STAGE"/*.txt "$STAGE"/*.ps1 "$STAGE"/*.md; do sed -i 's/\r$//; s/$/\r/' "$f"; done
( cd "$STAGE" && find Exporters -name '*.dll' | sort | xargs sha256sum > SHA256SUMS.txt )
( cd "$OUT" && rm -f "$NAME.zip" && zip -qr "$NAME.zip" "$NAME" )
( cd "$OUT" && sha256sum "$NAME.zip" > "$NAME.zip.sha256" )
echo "Package: $OUT/$NAME.zip"


===== FILE: build/update-package/ROLLBACK.cmd =====
@echo off
setlocal EnableExtensions DisableDelayedExpansion
set "O3D_PS=%SystemRoot%\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
if defined PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432 set "O3D_PS=%SystemRoot%\Sysnative\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
"%O3D_PS%" -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0Update-Offline3DExporter.ps1" -Rollback
set "O3D_RESULT=%ERRORLEVEL%"
pause
exit /b %O3D_RESULT%


===== FILE: build/update-package/UPDATE.cmd =====

setlocal EnableExtensions DisableDelayedExpansion
set "O3D_PS=%SystemRoot%\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
if defined PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432 set "O3D_PS=%SystemRoot%\Sysnative\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"

exit /b %O3D_RESULT%


===== FILE: build/update-package/Update-Offline3DExporter.ps1 =====
# Offline 3D PDF - replace the installed Revit exporter DLLs with 0.5.3.
# Usage: UPDATE.cmd (or this script with no arguments) to update,
#        ROLLBACK.cmd (-Rollback) to put the previous DLLs back.
# Only the exporter DLL that each Revit add-in manifest already points to is
# replaced. The viewer, the helper and the PDF format are not touched.
param([switch]$Rollback)
Set-StrictMode -Version 2
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$AddInId = '347FDBDE-BA6B-44D8-877F-9BFDA4CC2929'
$here = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path

function Get-TargetFramework([string]$Dll) {
    # The TargetFrameworkAttribute string is stored as plain text in the metadata.
    $text = [Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetString([IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($Dll))
    if ($text.Contains('.NETCoreApp,Version=v10.0')) { return 'net10.0-windows' }
    if ($text.Contains('.NETCoreApp,Version=v8.0')) { return 'net8.0-windows' }
    if ($text.Contains('.NETFramework,Version=v4.8')) { return 'net48' }
    return $null
}
function Get-Sha256([string]$Path) { return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() }

if (Get-Process -Name 'Revit' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) { throw 'Close every Revit window first, then run this again.' }

$sums = @{}
foreach ($line in Get-Content -LiteralPath ([IO.Path]::Combine($here, 'SHA256SUMS.txt'))) {
    if ($line -match '^([0-9a-f]{64})\s+\*?(.+)$') { $sums[$Matches[2].Replace('\', '/')] = $Matches[1] }
}

$appData = [Environment]::GetFolderPath('ApplicationData')
$changed = 0
foreach ($year in 2024..2027) {
    $manifest = [IO.Path]::Combine($appData, 'Autodesk', 'Revit', 'Addins', [string]$year, 'Offline3DPdfPrototype.addin')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $manifest)) { continue }
    $xml = New-Object xml; $xml.XmlResolver = $null; $xml.Load($manifest)
    $addIn = $xml.RevitAddIns.AddIn
    if ($addIn.AddInId -ne $AddInId) { Write-Warning ("Revit {0}: manifest is not Offline 3D PDF; skipped." -f $year); continue }
    $target = [string]$addIn.Assembly
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target)) { Write-Warning ("Revit {0}: installed exporter not found at {1}; skipped." -f $year, $target); continue }
    $backup = $target + '.v0.5.2.bak'
    $pdb = [IO.Path]::ChangeExtension($target, '.pdb')

    if ($Rollback) {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup) {
            Copy-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Destination $target -Force
            if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $pdb) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $pdb }
            Write-Host ("Revit {0}: previous exporter restored." -f $year); $changed++
        } else { Write-Host ("Revit {0}: no backup found; nothing to restore." -f $year) }
        continue
    }

    $framework = Get-TargetFramework $target
    if (-not $framework -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup)) { $framework = Get-TargetFramework $backup }
    if (-not $framework) { Write-Warning ("Revit {0}: could not tell which .NET version the installed exporter uses; skipped." -f $year); continue }
    $relative = 'Exporters/' + $year + '-' + $framework + '/Offline3DPrototype.Revit' + $year + '.dll'
    $source = [IO.Path]::Combine($here, 'Exporters', ([string]$year + '-' + $framework), ('Offline3DPrototype.Revit' + $year + '.dll'))
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $source)) { Write-Warning ("Revit {0}: no 0.5.3 build for {1}; skipped." -f $year, $framework); continue }
    if (-not $sums.ContainsKey($relative) -or $sums[$relative] -ne (Get-Sha256 $source)) { throw ('Checksum mismatch for ' + $relative + '. Download the package again.') }
    $helper = [IO.Path]::Combine((Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $target))), 'Viewer', 'Offline3D.FastPDF.Dev.exe')
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $helper)) { Write-Warning ("Revit {0}: the viewer helper is not at {1}; exports need it. Reinstall Offline 3D PDF if exports report it missing." -f $year, $helper) }

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup)) { Copy-Item -LiteralPath $target -Destination $backup }
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $source -Destination $target -Force
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath ([IO.Path]::ChangeExtension($source, '.pdb')) -Destination $pdb -Force
    foreach ($file in $target, $pdb) { try { Unblock-File -LiteralPath $file } catch { } }
    if ((Get-Sha256 $target) -ne $sums[$relative]) { Copy-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Destination $target -Force; throw ('Copy verification failed for Revit ' + $year + '; the previous exporter was restored.') }
    Write-Host ("Revit {0} ({1}): exporter updated to 0.5.3. Backup: {2}" -f $year, $framework, $backup); $changed++
}
if ($changed -eq 0) { Write-Host 'Nothing was changed. Is Offline 3D PDF installed for this Windows user?' }
elseif ($Rollback) { Write-Host ''; Write-Host 'Done. Start Revit: the previous exporter is back.' }
else { Write-Host ''; Write-Host 'Done. Start Revit: the ribbon panel now reads "Offline 3D PDF v0.5.3".' }

===== FILE: src/AutomaticRegistration/AutomaticRegistrationService.cs =====
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
namespace Offline3D.Automatic;
public sealed class AutomaticRegistrationResult
{
    public required CalibrationInput Controls {get;init;}
    public required CalibrationResult Calibration {get;init;}
    public required string AppearanceSha256 {get;init;}
    public int RenderWidth {get;init;} public int RenderHeight {get;init;}
    public int MarkerCount {get;init;}
}
public static class AutomaticRegistrationService
{
    private static readonly object Gate=new();
    public static AutomaticRegistrationResult Register(string cleanPdf,string markedPdf,Registration registration,SheetMarker[] markers) {
        if(markers.Length!=25||markers.Select(m=>m.Id).Distinct().Count()!=25||markers.Any(m=>m.Id<0||m.Id>=25||m.Red!=241||m.Blue!=229||m.Green!=7+9*m.Id))throw new InvalidDataException("Expected 25 valid registration markers");
        foreach(string p in new[]{cleanPdf,markedPdf})if(new FileInfo(p).Length>Contract.MaxPdf)throw new InvalidDataException("PDF exceeds bounded export budget");
        // PDFium is process-global and not thread safe; serialized, out of the Revit process.
        lock(Gate){Native.FPDF_InitLibrary();IntPtr clean=IntPtr.Zero,marked=IntPtr.Zero,cleanPage=IntPtr.Zero,markedPage=IntPtr.Zero;
            try{
                clean=Native.FPDF_LoadDocument(cleanPdf,IntPtr.Zero);marked=Native.FPDF_LoadDocument(markedPdf,IntPtr.Zero);
                if(clean==IntPtr.Zero||marked==IntPtr.Zero||Native.FPDF_GetPageCount(clean)!=1||Native.FPDF_GetPageCount(marked)!=1)throw new InvalidDataException("Two valid single-page native exports required");
                cleanPage=Native.FPDF_LoadPage(clean,0);markedPage=Native.FPDF_LoadPage(marked,0);
                if(cleanPage==IntPtr.Zero||markedPage==IntPtr.Zero)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot load exported page");
                var cleanGeometry=new Registration();var markedGeometry=new Registration();PdfPackageService.SetPageGeometry(cleanPdf,cleanGeometry);PdfPackageService.SetPageGeometry(markedPdf,markedGeometry);
                if(!cleanGeometry.MediaBox.SequenceEqual(markedGeometry.MediaBox)||!cleanGeometry.PageCropBox.SequenceEqual(markedGeometry.PageCropBox)||cleanGeometry.Rotation!=markedGeometry.Rotation||cleanGeometry.UserUnit!=markedGeometry.UserUnit)throw new InvalidDataException("Clean/marked export page geometry differs");
                var detected=markers.ToDictionary(m=>m.Id,_=>new List<Point3>());int objectBudget=0;
                void Visit(IntPtr owner,bool form,Matrix4 parent,int depth,bool remove){
                    if(depth>16)throw new InvalidDataException("PDF object nesting exceeds budget");
                    int count=form?Native.FPDFFormObj_CountObjects(owner):Native.FPDFPage_CountObjects(owner);
                    for(int i=count-1;i>=0;i--){if(++objectBudget>500000)throw new InvalidDataException("PDF object budget exceeded");
                        IntPtr obj=form?Native.FPDFFormObj_GetObject(owner,(uint)i):Native.FPDFPage_GetObject(owner,i);
                        if(Native.FPDFPageObj_GetMatrix(obj,out var local)==0)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot read PDF object transform");var matrix=parent*local.ToMatrix();int type=Native.FPDFPageObj_GetType(obj);
                        if(type==5){Visit(obj,true,matrix,depth+1,remove);continue;}
                        if(type!=2||Native.FPDFPageObj_GetStrokeColor(obj,out uint r,out uint g,out uint b,out uint a)==0)continue;
                        var matches=markers.Where(m=>Math.Abs((long)r-m.Red)<=1&&Math.Abs((long)g-m.Green)<=1&&Math.Abs((long)b-m.Blue)<=1&&a==255).ToArray();if(matches.Length==0)continue;
                        if(matches.Length!=1)throw new InvalidDataException("Ambiguous marker color");
                        if(!remove)throw new InvalidDataException("Drawing uses reserved calibration colors; change those line colors before export");
                        var points=detected[matches[0].Id];int segments=Native.FPDFPath_CountSegments(obj);if(segments<2||segments>16)throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported calibration path");
                        for(int k=0;k<segments;k++){if(Native.FPDFPathSegment_GetPoint(Native.FPDFPath_GetPathSegment(obj,k),out float x,out float y)==0)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid calibration vertex");points.Add(matrix.Apply(new Point3(x,y)));}
                        int removed=form?Native.FPDFFormObj_RemoveObject(owner,obj):Native.FPDFPage_RemoveObject(owner,obj);if(removed==0)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot isolate calibration marks");Native.FPDFPageObj_Destroy(obj);
                    }
                }
                Visit(cleanPage,false,Matrix4.Identity,0,false);Visit(markedPage,false,Matrix4.Identity,0,true);
                var controls=new List<ControlPoint>();
                foreach(var marker in markers.OrderBy(m=>m.Id)){
                    var unique=new List<Point3>();foreach(var p in detected[marker.Id])if(!unique.Any(q=>(p-q).Length<.0005))unique.Add(p);
                    if(unique.Count!=3)throw new InvalidDataException($"Marker {marker.Id+1}: expected exactly three L vertices, found {unique.Count}");
                    var candidates=unique.Where(p=>{var ends=unique.Where(q=>(q-p).Length>.0005).ToArray();var u=ends[0]-p;var v=ends[1]-p;return Math.Abs(u.X*v.X+u.Y*v.Y)/(u.Length*v.Length)<.01;}).ToArray();
                    if(candidates.Length!=1)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot identify calibration corner");
                    controls.Add(new ControlPoint{SheetX=marker.SheetX,SheetY=marker.SheetY,PdfX=candidates[0].X,PdfY=candidates[0].Y});
                }
                var input=new CalibrationInput{Fit=controls.Where((_,i)=>i%2==1).ToArray(),Check=controls.Where((_,i)=>i%2==0).ToArray()};
                var calibration=Calibration.Fit(input,registration.ModelToSheet,registration.LevelProjectElevationFeet,registration.ClickPlaneOrigin,registration.ClickPlaneNormal);
                // Compare the marked export after removing only the known markers with the untouched
                // clean native PDF. Deliver the clean PDF, so no content regeneration affects its drawing.
                if(Native.FPDFPage_GenerateContent(markedPage)==0)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot regenerate comparison page");
                float w=Native.FPDF_GetPageWidthF(cleanPage),h=Native.FPDF_GetPageHeightF(cleanPage);if(w<=0||h<=0||w>100000||h>100000)throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported page dimensions");
                double scale=Math.Min(2,Math.Sqrt(12000000d/(w*h)));int width=Math.Max(1,(int)Math.Round(w*scale)),height=Math.Max(1,(int)Math.Round(h*scale));
                byte[] original=Render(cleanPage,width,height),comparison=Render(markedPage,width,height);
                if(!original.SequenceEqual(comparison))throw new InvalidDataException("Clean/marked exports do not render identically after marker removal; no precise package was produced");
                return new AutomaticRegistrationResult{Controls=input,Calibration=calibration,AppearanceSha256=Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(original)).ToLowerInvariant(),RenderWidth=width,RenderHeight=height,MarkerCount=25};
            }finally{if(cleanPage!=IntPtr.Zero)Native.FPDF_ClosePage(cleanPage);if(markedPage!=IntPtr.Zero)Native.FPDF_ClosePage(markedPage);if(clean!=IntPtr.Zero)Native.FPDF_CloseDocument(clean);if(marked!=IntPtr.Zero)Native.FPDF_CloseDocument(marked);Native.FPDF_DestroyLibrary();}
        }
    }
    private static byte[] Render(IntPtr page,int width,int height){
        IntPtr bitmap=Native.FPDFBitmap_Create(width,height,0);if(bitmap==IntPtr.Zero)throw new InvalidDataException("Cannot allocate comparison bitmap");
        try{Native.FPDFBitmap_FillRect(bitmap,0,0,width,height,0xffffffff);Native.FPDF_RenderPageBitmap(bitmap,page,0,0,width,height,0,1);int stride=Native.FPDFBitmap_GetStride(bitmap);var bytes=new byte[checked(width*height*4)];IntPtr source=Native.FPDFBitmap_GetBuffer(bitmap);for(int y=0;y<height;y++)Marshal.Copy(source+y*stride,bytes,y*width*4,width*4);return bytes;}finally{Native.FPDFBitmap_Destroy(bitmap);}
    }
}


===== FILE: src/AutomaticRegistration/ExportFinalizer.cs =====
namespace Offline3D.Automatic;
public static class ExportFinalizer
{
    public static void Export(string folder,string output){
            string dir=Path.GetFullPath(folder),destination=Path.GetFullPath(output);
            if(!Path.GetFileName(dir).StartsWith("Offline3DExport-",StringComparison.Ordinal))throw new InvalidDataException("Unexpected export workspace");
            var reg=Contract.Decode<Registration>(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(dir,"registration.draft.json")));
            var markers=Contract.Decode<SheetMarker[]>(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(dir,"markers.json")));
            string clean=Path.Combine(dir,"drawing-clean.pdf");
            var result=Offline3D.Automatic.AutomaticRegistrationService.Register(clean,Path.Combine(dir,"drawing-marked.pdf"),reg,markers);
            reg.ModelToPdf=result.Calibration.SheetToPdf*reg.ModelToSheet;
            reg.CropPdf=Enumerable.Range(0,4).SelectMany(i=>{var p=result.Calibration.SheetToPdf.Apply(new Point3(reg.CropSheet[2*i],reg.CropSheet[2*i+1]));return new[]{p.X,p.Y};}).ToArray();
            foreach(var region in reg.ViewRegions){region.ModelToPdf=result.Calibration.SheetToPdf*region.ModelToSheet;region.CropPdf=Enumerable.Range(0,4).SelectMany(i=>{var p=result.Calibration.SheetToPdf.Apply(new Point3(region.CropSheet[2*i],region.CropSheet[2*i+1]));return new[]{p.X,p.Y};}).ToArray();}
            reg.PreciseClicks=true;reg.CalibrationEvidence=result.Controls;reg.FitPointCount=result.Controls.Fit.Length;reg.CheckPointCount=result.Controls.Check.Length;reg.MaxResidualMetres=result.Calibration.MaxResidualMetres;
            reg.AutomaticRegistration=result;PdfPackageService.SetPageGeometry(clean,reg);
            var files=new Dictionary<string,byte[]>{{"registration.json",Contract.Encode(reg)},{"identities.json",File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(dir,"identities.json"))},{"scene.glb",File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(dir,"scene.glb"))}};
            var exclusions=File.ReadAllLines(Path.Combine(dir,"exclusions.txt"));var coverage=exclusions.FirstOrDefault(x=>x.StartsWith("Coverage:",StringComparison.Ordinal))??"Mapped model view";var manifest=PdfPackageService.Describe(File.Exists(Path.Combine(dir,"synthetic.flag")),coverage+" Automatic 25-marker registration; 12 fit, 13 independent checks; clean appearance verified",File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(dir,"exporter-version.txt")),files,exclusions);
            using(var scene=System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse(files["scene.glb"].AsMemory(20,BitConverter.ToInt32(files["scene.glb"],12))))if(scene.RootElement.GetProperty("nodes").EnumerateArray().Any(n=>n.TryGetProperty("matrix",out _)))manifest.RequiredCapabilities=manifest.RequiredCapabilities.Concat(new[]{"glb-affine-instances-v1"}).ToArray();
            new PdfPackageService().Write(clean,destination,manifest,files);
            if(OperatingSystem.IsWindows())try{string logs=Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData),"Offline3DPdfPrototype","Logs","Exports",DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss")+"-"+manifest.PackageId);Directory.CreateDirectory(logs);foreach(var name in new[]{"geometry-diagnostics.txt","export-memory.txt","export-timings.txt","preview-timings.txt","preview-errors.txt","export-warnings.txt","unloaded-links.txt","exporter-version.txt"}){string file=Path.Combine(dir,name);if(File.Exists(file))File.Copy(file,Path.Combine(logs,name));}}catch(IOException){}catch(UnauthorizedAccessException){}
            try{Directory.Delete(dir,true);}catch(IOException){}catch(UnauthorizedAccessException){}
    }
}


===== FILE: src/AutomaticRegistration/Native.cs =====
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
namespace Offline3D.Automatic;
internal static class Native
{
    const string Dll="offline3d_pdfium";
    static Native(){NativeLibrary.SetDllImportResolver(typeof(Native).Assembly,Resolve);}
    private static IntPtr Resolve(string name,Assembly assembly,DllImportSearchPath? path){
        if(name!=Dll)return IntPtr.Zero;string file=OperatingSystem.IsWindows()?"pdfium.dll":"libpdfium.so";
        foreach(string p in new[]{Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory,file),Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory,"runtimes",OperatingSystem.IsWindows()?"win-x64":"linux-x64","native",file)})
            if(File.Exists(p))return NativeLibrary.Load(p);
        throw new DllNotFoundException("Packaged PDFium dependency missing: "+file);
    }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]internal struct Matrix {public float A,B,C,D,E,F;public Matrix4 ToMatrix()=>new(A,C,0,E,B,D,0,F,0,0,1,0,0,0,0,1);}
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDF_InitLibrary();
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDF_DestroyLibrary();
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDF_LoadDocument([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPUTF8Str)]string file,IntPtr password);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDF_CloseDocument(IntPtr doc);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDF_GetPageCount(IntPtr doc);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDF_LoadPage(IntPtr doc,int index);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDF_ClosePage(IntPtr page);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern float FPDF_GetPageWidthF(IntPtr page);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern float FPDF_GetPageHeightF(IntPtr page);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPage_CountObjects(IntPtr page);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDFPage_GetObject(IntPtr page,int index);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPageObj_GetType(IntPtr obj);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPageObj_GetMatrix(IntPtr obj,out Matrix matrix);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFFormObj_CountObjects(IntPtr form);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDFFormObj_GetObject(IntPtr form,uint index);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFFormObj_RemoveObject(IntPtr form,IntPtr obj);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPage_RemoveObject(IntPtr page,IntPtr obj);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDFPageObj_Destroy(IntPtr obj);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPage_GenerateContent(IntPtr page);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPageObj_GetStrokeColor(IntPtr obj,out uint r,out uint g,out uint b,out uint a);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPath_CountSegments(IntPtr obj);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDFPath_GetPathSegment(IntPtr obj,int index);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFPathSegment_GetPoint(IntPtr segment,out float x,out float y);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDFBitmap_Create(int width,int height,int alpha);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDFBitmap_Destroy(IntPtr bitmap);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFBitmap_FillRect(IntPtr bitmap,int left,int top,int width,int height,uint color);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern void FPDF_RenderPageBitmap(IntPtr bitmap,IntPtr page,int x,int y,int w,int h,int rotate,int flags);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern IntPtr FPDFBitmap_GetBuffer(IntPtr bitmap);
    [DllImport(Dll,CallingConvention=CallingConvention.Cdecl)]internal static extern int FPDFBitmap_GetStride(IntPtr bitmap);
}


===== FILE: src/Core/AutomaticExport.cs =====
namespace Offline3D;
public sealed class SheetMarker
{
    public int Id {get;set;}
    public double SheetX {get;set;} public double SheetY {get;set;}
    public int Red {get;set;}=241; public int Green {get;set;} public int Blue {get;set;}=229;
}
public static class AutomaticExport
{
    public static SheetMarker[] Markers(double[] cropSheet) {
        if(cropSheet.Length!=8)throw new InvalidDataException("One rectangular sheet crop required");
        var markers=new List<SheetMarker>();
        for(int y=0;y<5;y++)for(int x=0;x<5;x++){
            double u=.08+.21*x,v=.08+.21*y;int i=y*5+x;
            markers.Add(new SheetMarker{Id=i,Green=7+9*i,SheetX=cropSheet[0]+u*(cropSheet[2]-cropSheet[0])+v*(cropSheet[6]-cropSheet[0]),SheetY=cropSheet[1]+u*(cropSheet[3]-cropSheet[1])+v*(cropSheet[7]-cropSheet[1])});
        }return markers.ToArray();
    }
}


===== FILE: src/Core/BoxVolume.cs =====
namespace Offline3D;

/// <summary>Axis-aligned section bounds in local graphics metres.</summary>
public sealed class BoxVolume
{
    public double MinX{get;set;} public double MaxX{get;set;}
    public double MinY{get;set;} public double MaxY{get;set;}
    public double MinZ{get;set;} public double MaxZ{get;set;}
    public double Level{get;set;}
    public double Width=>MaxX-MinX;
    public double Depth=>MaxZ-MinZ;
    public double Above=>MaxY-Level;
    public double Below=>Level-MinY;
    public void Validate(){
        if(new[]{MinX,MaxX,MinY,MaxY,MinZ,MaxZ,Level}.Any(v=>double.IsNaN(v)||double.IsInfinity(v)||Math.Abs(v)>10000000)||Width<.01||Width>20000000||Depth<.01||Depth>20000000||Above<.01||Above>20000000||Below<0||Below>20000000)
            throw new InvalidDataException("Box bounds must be finite with positive dimensions; below level may be zero.");
    }
    public static BoxVolume Stock(Point3 point)=>Centered(point,3.048,3.048,3.048,.1524);
    public static BoxVolume Centered(Point3 p,double width,double depth,double above,double below){var box=new BoxVolume{MinX=p.X-width/2,MaxX=p.X+width/2,MinY=p.Y-below,MaxY=p.Y+above,MinZ=p.Z-depth/2,MaxZ=p.Z+depth/2,Level=p.Y};box.Validate();return box;}
    public Point3[] PdfFootprint(Registration registration){
        Validate();var toModel=registration.ModelToGraphics.Inverse();
        return new[]{new Point3(MinX,Level,MinZ),new Point3(MaxX,Level,MinZ),new Point3(MaxX,Level,MaxZ),new Point3(MinX,Level,MaxZ)}.Select(p=>registration.ModelToPdf.Apply(toModel.Apply(p))).ToArray();
    }
}


===== FILE: src/Core/Contract.cs =====
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
namespace Offline3D;
public static class Contract
{
    public const string ManifestName="offline3d.manifest.json";
    public const int MaxResource=SceneLimits.MaxMetadataBytes,MaxScene=SceneLimits.MaxSceneBytes,MaxTotal=SceneLimits.MaxTotalBytes,MaxPdf=SceneLimits.MaxPdfBytes;
    public static readonly JsonSerializerOptions Json=new JsonSerializerOptions{PropertyNamingPolicy=JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase,PropertyNameCaseInsensitive=false,WriteIndented=true,MaxDepth=32};
    public static byte[] Encode<T>(T value)=>JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(value,Json);
    public static byte[] EncodeExact<T>(T value)=>JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(value);
    public static T Decode<T>(byte[] data) {if(data.Length>MaxResource)throw new InvalidDataException("JSON too large");return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<T>(data,Json)??throw new InvalidDataException("Null JSON");}
    public static string Hash(byte[] data){using(var sha=SHA256.Create())return BitConverter.ToString(sha.ComputeHash(data)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();}
    public static void Validate(Manifest m) {
        if(m==null || m.SchemaVersion!=1 || !Guid.TryParse(m.PackageId,out _) || m.CoordinateFrame!="revit-host-internal-feet" || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(m.Coverage))throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported/invalid manifest");
        if(m.RequiredCapabilities==null || m.RequiredCapabilities.Any(c=>c!="plan-ray-v1"&&c!="glb-static-v1"&&c!="glb-affine-instances-v1"&&c!=SceneLimits.LargeSceneCapability))throw new InvalidDataException("Unknown required capability");
        if(m.Resources==null || m.Resources.Length!=3)throw new InvalidDataException("Expected registration, identities and one scene");
        if(m.Resources.Select(r=>r.Id).Distinct().Count()!=3 || m.Resources.Select(r=>r.Name).Distinct().Count()!=3)throw new InvalidDataException("Duplicate resource");
        foreach(var r in m.Resources)if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(r.Id)||r.Id.Length>100 || !System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(r.Name??"",@"^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\.(json|glb)$") || r.Name==ManifestName || r.Size<1 || r.Size>SceneLimits.ResourceBytes(r.Kind) || !System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(r.Sha256??"","^[a-f0-9]{64}$"))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid resource");
        if(m.Resources.Any(r=>r.Kind=="scene"&&r.Size>MaxResource)&&!m.RequiredCapabilities.Contains(SceneLimits.LargeSceneCapability))throw new InvalidDataException("Large scenes must declare viewer support");
        if(m.Resources.Sum(r=>r.Size)>MaxTotal || !new[]{"registration","identities","scene"}.OrderBy(x=>x).SequenceEqual(m.Resources.Select(r=>r.Kind).OrderBy(x=>x)))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid resource kinds/total");
    }
    public static void Validate(Registration r) {
        bool Finite(double v)=>!double.IsNaN(v)&&!double.IsInfinity(v);
        if(r.Page!=1 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(r.ViewId)||string.IsNullOrEmpty(r.LevelId)||string.IsNullOrEmpty(r.DocumentId)||!Finite(r.LevelProjectElevationFeet))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid view/level");
        foreach(var matrix in new[]{r.ModelToPdf,r.ModelToSheet,r.ModelToGraphics}){matrix.Validate();matrix.Inverse();if(!matrix.Values.Skip(12).SequenceEqual(new[]{0d,0,0,1}))throw new InvalidDataException("Only affine frames supported");}
        foreach(var box in new[]{r.MediaBox,r.PageCropBox})if(box==null||box.Length!=4||box.Any(x=>!Finite(x))||box[2]<=box[0]||box[3]<=box[1])throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid page box");
        if(r.Rotation%90!=0 || r.Rotation<0 ||r.Rotation>270 || !Finite(r.UserUnit)||r.UserUnit<=0||r.UserUnit>75000)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid page transform");
        if(r.CropPdf==null||r.CropPdf.Length!=8||r.CropPdf.Any(x=>!Finite(x)))throw new InvalidDataException("Only rectangular unsplit crops supported");
        // Convex quadrilateral; crop is in PDF coordinates and may be rotated.
        double sign=0;for(int i=0;i<4;i++){int j=(i+1)%4,k=(i+2)%4;double cross=(r.CropPdf[2*j]-r.CropPdf[2*i])*(r.CropPdf[2*k+1]-r.CropPdf[2*j+1])-(r.CropPdf[2*j+1]-r.CropPdf[2*i+1])*(r.CropPdf[2*k]-r.CropPdf[2*j]);if(Math.Abs(cross)<1e-12 || (i>0&&cross*sign<=0))throw new InvalidDataException("Degenerate crop");sign=cross;}
        if(!r.PreciseClicks || !Finite(r.MaxResidualMetres)||r.MaxResidualMetres<0||r.MaxResidualMetres>.001||r.FitPointCount<3||r.CheckPointCount<3)throw new InvalidDataException("Precise interaction requires verified registration");
        if(r.ViewRegions==null||r.ViewRegions.Length>100)throw new InvalidDataException("Too many sheet regions");
        void Plane(double[] o,double[] n){if(o.Length==0&&n.Length==0)return;if(o.Length!=3||n.Length!=3||o.Concat(n).Any(v=>!Finite(v))||n.Sum(v=>v*v)<.5)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid click plane");}
        void Bounds(double[] bounds){if(bounds.Length==0)return;if(bounds.Length!=6||bounds.Any(v=>!Finite(v)||Math.Abs(v)>1e9)||Enumerable.Range(0,3).Any(i=>bounds[i+3]<=bounds[i]))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid scene bounds");}
        Plane(r.ClickPlaneOrigin,r.ClickPlaneNormal);Bounds(r.ExportBoundsGraphics);
        foreach(var region in r.ViewRegions){region.ModelToPdf.Validate();region.ModelToPdf.Inverse();Plane(region.PlaneOrigin,region.PlaneNormal);Bounds(region.BoundsGraphics);if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(region.ViewId)||region.CropPdf.Length!=8||region.CropPdf.Any(v=>!Finite(v)))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid view region");}
        Coordinates.PlanClick(r,r.CropPdf[0],r.CropPdf[1]);
        // Graphics mapping is the declared invertible feet -> metres, Z-up -> Y-up axis conversion.
        var a=r.ModelToGraphics.Values;var expected=Matrix4.Graphics(new Point3()).Values;
        for(int i=0;i<16;i++)if(i!=3&&i!=7&&i!=11&&Math.Abs(a[i]-expected[i])>1e-10)throw new InvalidDataException("Unexpected graphics axes/scale");
    }
    public static void ValidateGlb(byte[] bytes) {
        if(bytes.Length<28||bytes.Length>MaxScene||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,0)!=0x46546c67||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,4)!=2||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,8)!=bytes.Length)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid GLB header");
        int n=checked((int)BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,12));if(n<2||n>MaxResource||n>bytes.Length-28||n%4!=0||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,16)!=0x4e4f534a)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid GLB JSON chunk");
        using(var doc=JsonDocument.Parse(new ReadOnlyMemory<byte>(bytes,20,n))){
            void Scan(JsonElement e,bool metadata=false){if(e.ValueKind==JsonValueKind.Number && (!e.TryGetDouble(out var number)||double.IsNaN(number)||double.IsInfinity(number)))throw new InvalidDataException("Non-finite GLB value");if(e.ValueKind==JsonValueKind.Object)foreach(var p in e.EnumerateObject()){if(!metadata&&(p.Name=="uri"||p.Name=="extensions"||p.Name=="extensionsRequired"||p.Name=="extensionsUsed"||p.Name=="images"||p.Name=="animations"||p.Name=="skins"))throw new InvalidDataException("GLB must be static and self-contained, without extensions/images");Scan(p.Value,metadata||p.Name=="extras");}else if(e.ValueKind==JsonValueKind.Array)foreach(var v in e.EnumerateArray())Scan(v,metadata);}
            Scan(doc.RootElement);
            if(doc.RootElement.GetProperty("asset").GetProperty("version").GetString()!="2.0")throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported glTF");
            var buffers=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("buffers");if(buffers.GetArrayLength()!=1)throw new InvalidDataException("Expected one local binary buffer");
            int offset=20+n; if(offset+8>bytes.Length||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,offset+4)!=0x004e4942||BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,offset)!=bytes.Length-offset-8 || buffers[0].GetProperty("byteLength").GetInt32()>bytes.Length-offset-8)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid GLB BIN chunk");
            int binaryLength=buffers[0].GetProperty("byteLength").GetInt32();
            if(binaryLength<0)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid binary length");
            var views=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("bufferViews");var accessors=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("accessors");
            if(views.GetArrayLength()>300000||accessors.GetArrayLength()>300000)throw new InvalidDataException("Too many geometry accessors");
            foreach(var view in views.EnumerateArray()){
                long start=view.TryGetProperty("byteOffset",out var off)?off.GetInt64():0,length=view.GetProperty("byteLength").GetInt64();
                if(view.GetProperty("buffer").GetInt32()!=0||start<0||start>binaryLength||length<0||length>binaryLength-start||view.TryGetProperty("byteStride",out _))throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported/out-of-range buffer view");
            }
            foreach(var accessor in accessors.EnumerateArray()){
                int viewIndex=accessor.GetProperty("bufferView").GetInt32(),count=accessor.GetProperty("count").GetInt32(),component=accessor.GetProperty("componentType").GetInt32();
                string type=accessor.GetProperty("type").GetString()??"";
                bool vector=component==5126&&type=="VEC3",index=component==5125&&type=="SCALAR";
                if(viewIndex<0||viewIndex>=views.GetArrayLength()||count<1||(!vector&&!index)||count>(vector?3000000:16000000)||accessor.TryGetProperty("sparse",out _)||(accessor.TryGetProperty("normalized",out var normalized)&&normalized.GetBoolean()))throw new InvalidDataException("Only bounded float VEC3 or uint32 SCALAR accessors supported");
                long relative=accessor.TryGetProperty("byteOffset",out var off)?off.GetInt64():0;
                var view=views[viewIndex];long viewLength=view.GetProperty("byteLength").GetInt64(),width=vector?12:4;
                if(relative<0||relative%4!=0||relative>viewLength||(long)count*width>viewLength-relative)throw new InvalidDataException("Accessor exceeds buffer");
                int start=checked((int)((view.TryGetProperty("byteOffset",out var vo)?vo.GetInt64():0)+relative));
                if(start%4!=0)throw new InvalidDataException("Misaligned geometry accessor");
                if(vector)for(int k=0;k<count*3;k++){float value=BitConverter.ToSingle(bytes,offset+8+start+k*4);if(float.IsNaN(value)||float.IsInfinity(value))throw new InvalidDataException("Non-finite GLB vertex");}
            }
            int AccessorStart(int index){var a=accessors[index];var v=views[a.GetProperty("bufferView").GetInt32()];return checked((int)((v.TryGetProperty("byteOffset",out var vo)?vo.GetInt64():0)+(a.TryGetProperty("byteOffset",out var ao)?ao.GetInt64():0)));}
            var meshes=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("meshes");
            var hasMaterials=doc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("materials",out var materials);
            if(hasMaterials){
                if(materials.GetArrayLength()>100000)throw new InvalidDataException("Too many materials");
                foreach(var material in materials.EnumerateArray()){
                    if(material.TryGetProperty("alphaMode",out var alpha)&&alpha.GetString()!="OPAQUE"&&alpha.GetString()!="BLEND"&&alpha.GetString()!="MASK")throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid material alpha mode");
                    if(material.TryGetProperty("pbrMetallicRoughness",out var pbr)){
                        if(pbr.TryGetProperty("baseColorFactor",out var color)&&(color.GetArrayLength()!=4||color.EnumerateArray().Any(v=>v.GetDouble()<0||v.GetDouble()>1)))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid material color");
                        foreach(var key in new[]{"roughnessFactor","metallicFactor"})if(pbr.TryGetProperty(key,out var value)&&(value.GetDouble()<0||value.GetDouble()>1))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid material surface value");
                    }
                }
            }
            if(meshes.GetArrayLength()<1||meshes.GetArrayLength()>100000)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid mesh count");
            var meshVertexCounts=new List<long>();long totalMeshVertices=0;
            foreach(var mesh in meshes.EnumerateArray()){
                long meshVertices=0;
                foreach(var primitive in mesh.GetProperty("primitives").EnumerateArray()){
                    if(primitive.GetProperty("mode").GetInt32()!=4)throw new InvalidDataException("Only triangles supported");
                    if(primitive.TryGetProperty("material",out var materialIndex)&&(!hasMaterials||materialIndex.GetInt32()<0||materialIndex.GetInt32()>=materials.GetArrayLength()))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid material index");
                    var attributes=primitive.GetProperty("attributes");var names=new HashSet<string>();
                    int positionIndex=attributes.GetProperty("POSITION").GetInt32();
                    if(positionIndex<0||positionIndex>=accessors.GetArrayLength())throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid position accessor");
                    int vertexCount=accessors[positionIndex].GetProperty("count").GetInt32();
                    foreach(var attribute in attributes.EnumerateObject()){
                        int ai=attribute.Value.GetInt32();
                        if(!names.Add(attribute.Name)||(attribute.Name!="POSITION"&&attribute.Name!="NORMAL")||ai<0||ai>=accessors.GetArrayLength())throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported mesh attribute");
                        var accessor=accessors[ai];
                        if(accessor.GetProperty("componentType").GetInt32()!=5126||accessor.GetProperty("type").GetString()!="VEC3"||accessor.GetProperty("count").GetInt32()!=vertexCount)throw new InvalidDataException("Mismatched geometry attributes");
                    }
                    if(primitive.TryGetProperty("indices",out var indexProperty)){
                        int ai=indexProperty.GetInt32();if(ai<0||ai>=accessors.GetArrayLength())throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid index accessor");
                        var accessor=accessors[ai];int count=accessor.GetProperty("count").GetInt32();
                        if(accessor.GetProperty("componentType").GetInt32()!=5125||accessor.GetProperty("type").GetString()!="SCALAR"||count%3!=0)throw new InvalidDataException("Only complete uint32 indexed triangles supported");
                        int start=AccessorStart(ai);for(int k=0;k<count;k++)if(BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,offset+8+start+k*4)>=vertexCount)throw new InvalidDataException("Triangle index exceeds vertex count");
                        meshVertices+=count;
                    }else{
                        if(vertexCount%3!=0)throw new InvalidDataException("Incomplete triangles");meshVertices+=vertexCount;
                    }
                    
                }
                if(mesh.TryGetProperty("extras",out var extra)&&extra.TryGetProperty("featureEdges",out var edge)){
                    int ei=edge.GetInt32();if(ei<0||ei>=accessors.GetArrayLength())throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid feature edges");var ea=accessors[ei];int count=ea.GetProperty("count").GetInt32();if(ea.GetProperty("componentType").GetInt32()!=5125||ea.GetProperty("type").GetString()!="SCALAR"||count%2!=0)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid edge pairs");int vertices=accessors[mesh.GetProperty("primitives")[0].GetProperty("attributes").GetProperty("POSITION").GetInt32()].GetProperty("count").GetInt32(),start=AccessorStart(ei);for(int k=0;k<count;k++)if(BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes,offset+8+start+k*4)>=vertices)throw new InvalidDataException("Feature edge outside vertices");
                }
                totalMeshVertices+=meshVertices;meshVertexCounts.Add(meshVertices);
            }
            // Flat mesh nodes only: excludes cyclic or arbitrarily deep scene graphs.
            var nodes=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("nodes");if(nodes.GetArrayLength()>100000)throw new InvalidDataException("Too many nodes");
            long instancedVertices=0;int elementCount=0;if(doc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("extras",out var rootExtras)&&rootExtras.TryGetProperty("revitElements",out var elementTable)){if(elementTable.ValueKind!=JsonValueKind.Array||elementTable.GetArrayLength()>100000)throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid element metadata table");elementCount=elementTable.GetArrayLength();}
            foreach(var node in nodes.EnumerateArray()){
                if(node.TryGetProperty("extras",out var nodeExtras)&&nodeExtras.TryGetProperty("element",out var elementRef)&&(elementRef.GetInt32()<0||elementRef.GetInt32()>=elementCount))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid element metadata reference");
                if(node.TryGetProperty("children",out _)||node.TryGetProperty("rotation",out _)||node.TryGetProperty("scale",out _))throw new InvalidDataException("Only flat, validated mesh placements are supported");
                if(node.TryGetProperty("matrix",out var transform)){
                    if(transform.GetArrayLength()!=16||node.TryGetProperty("translation",out _))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid placement matrix");
                    var column=transform.EnumerateArray().Select(v=>v.GetDouble()).ToArray();var frame=new Matrix4(Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>column[(i%4)*4+i/4]).ToArray());frame.Inverse();var f=frame.Values;
                    if(!f.Skip(12).SequenceEqual(new[]{0d,0,0,1})||new[]{f[3],f[7],f[11]}.Any(v=>Math.Abs(v)>1e7))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid affine placement");
                    var lengths=Enumerable.Range(0,3).Select(i=>Math.Sqrt(f[i]*f[i]+f[4+i]*f[4+i]+f[8+i]*f[8+i])).ToArray();if(lengths.Any(v=>v<1e-6||v>1e6))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid placement scale");
                    for(int i=0;i<3;i++)for(int j=i+1;j<3;j++)if(Math.Abs(f[i]*f[j]+f[4+i]*f[4+j]+f[8+i]*f[8+j])>1e-5*lengths[i]*lengths[j])throw new InvalidDataException("Sheared placements are unsupported");
                    double determinant=f[0]*(f[5]*f[10]-f[6]*f[9])-f[1]*(f[4]*f[10]-f[6]*f[8])+f[2]*(f[4]*f[9]-f[5]*f[8]);if(determinant<=0)throw new InvalidDataException("Mirrors must be stored in geometry with corrected winding");
                }
                else if(node.TryGetProperty("translation",out var translation)&&(translation.GetArrayLength()!=3||translation.EnumerateArray().Any(v=>Math.Abs(v.GetDouble())>1e7)))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid node translation");
                int index=node.GetProperty("mesh").GetInt32();if(index<0||index>=meshes.GetArrayLength())throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid mesh index");
                instancedVertices+=meshVertexCounts[index]; // Informational: repeated placements do not consume unique binary storage.
            }
            var scenes=doc.RootElement.GetProperty("scenes");if(scenes.GetArrayLength()!=1||doc.RootElement.GetProperty("scene").GetInt32()!=0)throw new InvalidDataException("One scene required");
            var sceneNodes=new HashSet<int>();foreach(var index in scenes[0].GetProperty("nodes").EnumerateArray())if(index.GetInt32()<0||index.GetInt32()>=nodes.GetArrayLength()||!sceneNodes.Add(index.GetInt32()))throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid or duplicate scene node");

        }
    }
}

namespace Offline3D;
public sealed class Resource
{
    public string Id {get;set;}=""; public string Name {get;set;}=""; public string Kind {get;set;}="";
    public long Size {get;set;} public string Sha256 {get;set;}="";
}
public sealed class Manifest
{
    public int SchemaVersion {get;set;}=1;
    public string PackageId {get;set;}=Guid.NewGuid().ToString("D");
    public string CoordinateFrame {get;set;}="revit-host-internal-feet";
    public string[] RequiredCapabilities {get;set;}=new[]{"plan-ray-v1","glb-static-v1"};
    public bool Synthetic {get;set;}
    public string Coverage {get;set;}="";
    public string ExporterVersion {get;set;}="";
    public string[] Exclusions {get;set;}=Array.Empty<string>();
    public Resource[] Resources {get;set;}=Array.Empty<Resource>();
}
public sealed class Registration
{
    public int Page {get;set;}=1;
    public double[] ClickPlaneOrigin {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] ClickPlaneNormal {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public ViewRegion[] ViewRegions {get;set;}=Array.Empty<ViewRegion>();
    public double[] ExportBoundsGraphics {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public string ViewId {get;set;}=""; public string LevelId {get;set;}=""; public string DocumentId {get;set;}="";
    public double LevelProjectElevationFeet {get;set;}
    public Matrix4 ModelToPdf {get;set;}=Matrix4.Identity;
    public Matrix4 ModelToSheet {get;set;}=Matrix4.Identity;
    public Matrix4 ModelToGraphics {get;set;}=Matrix4.Graphics(new Point3());
    public double[] CropPdf {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] CropSheet {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] MediaBox {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] PageCropBox {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public int Rotation {get;set;}
    public double UserUnit {get;set;}=1;
    public bool PreciseClicks {get;set;}
    public double MaxResidualMetres {get;set;}
    public int FitPointCount {get;set;} public int CheckPointCount {get;set;}
    public CalibrationInput? CalibrationEvidence {get;set;}
    public object? AutomaticRegistration {get;set;}
}

public sealed class ViewRegion
{
    public string ViewId {get;set;}="";
    public string Name {get;set;}="";
    public string Kind {get;set;}="";
    public Matrix4 ModelToSheet {get;set;}=Matrix4.Identity;
    public Matrix4 ModelToPdf {get;set;}=Matrix4.Identity;
    public double[] CropSheet {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] CropPdf {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] PlaneOrigin {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double[] PlaneNormal {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
    public double LevelFeet {get;set;}
    public double[] BoundsGraphics {get;set;}=Array.Empty<double>();
}
