        estimate.AutoSize = true; estimate.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Right; estimate.Font = new Font(Font, FontStyle.Bold); estimate.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, S(2)); Add(estimate);
        Add(SmallLabel("A rough estimate. The real size and triangle counts are measured during export.", 0));

        optionsScroller.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; optionsScroller.Margin = new Padding(0); optionsScroller.Padding = new Padding(0);
        optionsScroller.Controls.Add(options);
        // Vertical scrolling only: content is laid out to the panel's width.
        optionsScroller.AutoScroll = false;
        optionsScroller.HorizontalScroll.Enabled = false; optionsScroller.HorizontalScroll.Visible = false; optionsScroller.HorizontalScroll.Maximum = 0;
        optionsScroller.AutoScroll = true;
    }

    private void BuildFooter()
    {
        footer.ColumnCount = 2; footer.AutoSize = true; footer.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; footer.Margin = new Padding(0, S(10), 0, 0);
        footer.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100)); footer.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        status.AutoSize = false; status.AutoEllipsis = true; status.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; status.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft; status.ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(96, 96, 96);
        var buttons = new FlowLayoutPanel { AutoSize = true, WrapContents = false, FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight, Margin = new Padding(0) };
        foreach (var b in new[] { quickButton, exportButton, cancelButton }) { b.AutoSize = true; b.MinimumSize = new Size(S(104), S(32)); b.Margin = new Padding(S(8), 0, 0, 0); }
        quickButton.Text = "Quick export"; exportButton.Text = "Export…"; cancelButton.Text = "Cancel";
        exportButton.Font = new Font(Font, FontStyle.Bold);
        quickButton.Click += (_, __) => { detail.SelectedIndex = (int)ExportDetail.Quick; Finish(OptionsOutcome.Export); };
        exportButton.Click += (_, __) => Finish(OptionsOutcome.Export);
        cancelButton.Click += (_, __) => Finish(OptionsOutcome.Cancel);
        AcceptButton = exportButton; CancelButton = cancelButton;
        buttons.Controls.Add(quickButton); buttons.Controls.Add(exportButton); buttons.Controls.Add(cancelButton);
        footer.Controls.Add(status, 0, 0); footer.Controls.Add(buttons, 1, 0);
        var tip = new ToolTip();
        tip.SetToolTip(quickButton, "Export now with Quick detail: coarse geometry, colours and properties kept, no precomputed edges.");
    }

    /// <summary>Two columns when there is room; preview above options otherwise.</summary>
    private void ApplyLayout()
    {
        bool wantNarrow = ClientSize.Width < S(780);
        if (narrow == wantNarrow) return;
        narrow = wantNarrow;
        root.SuspendLayout();
        root.Controls.Clear(); root.ColumnStyles.Clear(); root.RowStyles.Clear();
        if (!wantNarrow)
        {
            root.ColumnCount = 2; root.RowCount = 3;
            root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100)); root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Absolute, S(330)));
            root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize)); root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100)); root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            root.Controls.Add(header, 0, 0); root.SetColumnSpan(header, 2);
            previewColumn.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, S(16), 0);
            root.Controls.Add(previewColumn, 0, 1); root.Controls.Add(optionsScroller, 1, 1);
            root.Controls.Add(footer, 0, 2); root.SetColumnSpan(footer, 2);
        }
        else
        {
            root.ColumnCount = 1; root.RowCount = 4;
            root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100));
            root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize)); root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 45)); root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 55)); root.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
            previewColumn.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, S(10));
            root.Controls.Add(header, 0, 0); root.Controls.Add(previewColumn, 0, 1); root.Controls.Add(optionsScroller, 0, 2); root.Controls.Add(footer, 0, 3);
        }
        root.ResumeLayout(true);
    }

    private void CenterOverlay()
    {
        overlay.Location = new Point(Math.Max(0, (previewHost.ClientSize.Width - overlay.Width) / 2), Math.Max(0, (previewHost.ClientSize.Height - overlay.Height) / 2));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ state

    private void ShowOverlay(string title, string text, string? button, bool busyState)
    {
        overlayTitle.Text = title; overlayText.Text = text;
        overlayBar.Visible = busyState;
        overlayButton.Visible = button != null; if (button != null) overlayButton.Text = button;
        overlay.Visible = true; overlay.BringToFront(); CenterOverlay();
    }

    private string BusyText() => "Revit is rendering the export area. This window stays open; the image appears here when Revit finishes (" + (int)(DateTime.Now - busySince).TotalSeconds + " s).";

    private void OptionsChanged()
    {
        UpdateScopeSummary(); UpdateEstimate();
        staleBanner.Visible = shownKey != null && shownKey != CurrentRequest().Key && !busy;
    }

    private void UpdateScopeSummary()
    {
        scopeSummary.Text = (OutsideCrop ? "Outside crop included (confirmed)" : "View crop") + "\nBottom: " + (AllDown ? "all levels below" : bottomLabel) + "\nTop: " + (AllUp ? "all levels above" : topLabel);
    }

    private void UpdateDetailHint()
    {
        detailHint.Text = DetailPreset switch
        {
            ExportDetail.Quick => "Coarse Revit geometry, colours and properties; no precomputed edges. Fastest.",
            ExportDetail.Low => "Fewer triangles on curved surfaces.",
            ExportDetail.Medium => "Standard Revit tessellation. Recommended for team review.",
            ExportDetail.High => "Fine Revit detail; larger files and longer export.",
            _ => "Maximum tessellation; can be slow and large."
        };
    }

    private void UpdateEstimate()
    {
        long perElement = new[] { 256L, 768L, 1536L, 3072L, 6144L }[(int)DetailPreset];
        double formatFactor = OutsideCrop ? 1 : AllUp || AllDown ? .6 : .35;
        double typical = 2 * 1048576d + candidateCount * perElement * formatFactor;
        long min = (long)Math.Max(1048576, typical * .35), max = (long)Math.Max(2 * 1048576, typical * 2.5);
        estimate.Text = FormatSize(min) + " – " + FormatSize(max);
    }

    private static string FormatSize(long bytes) => bytes >= 1073741824 ? (bytes / 1073741824d).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + " GB" : (bytes / 1048576d).ToString("0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + " MB";

    private PreviewRequest CurrentRequest() => new PreviewRequest { OutsideCrop = OutsideCrop, AllUp = AllUp, AllDown = AllDown, IncludeAllCategories = IncludeAllCategories, PixelSize = picture.ClientSize };

    private void RequestPreview() { if (!busy) previewWanted = true; }

    private void Finish(OptionsOutcome outcome)
    {
        if (busy) { if (outcome == OptionsOutcome.Cancel) { cancelAfterPreview = true; status.Text = "Cancelling after Revit finishes the preview…"; } return; }
        Outcome = outcome; finished = true;
    }

    private void SetBusy(bool value)
    {
        busy = value;
        foreach (var c in new Control[] { outsideCrop, categories, parameterNames, detail, skipTiny, previewButton, quickButton, exportButton }) c.Enabled = !value;
        allUp.Enabled = allDown.Enabled = !value && planHeight;
        tinySize.Enabled = !value && skipTiny.Checked;
        cancelButton.Enabled = true;
        if (value) { busySince = DateTime.Now; ticker.Start(); } else ticker.Stop();
        UseWaitCursor = value;
    }

    protected override void OnFormClosing(FormClosingEventArgs e)
    {
        // Closing with the window button means Cancel; the loop hides the form.
        if (!finished) { e.Cancel = true; Finish(OptionsOutcome.Cancel); }
        base.OnFormClosing(e);
    }

    protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.KeyCode == Keys.F5) { RequestPreview(); e.Handled = true; }
        base.OnKeyDown(e);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ running

    /// <param name="generatePreview">Renders the preview with the Revit API and
    /// returns the image path. Called from this method's loop only. The second
    /// argument reports progress text and keeps the window painted.</param>
    public OptionsOutcome RunModal(IWin32Window owner, Func<PreviewRequest, Action<string>, string> generatePreview)
    {
        var area = NativeWindows.WorkingArea(owner.Handle, out var ownerBounds);
        int width = Math.Min(S(1080), area.Width - S(32)), height = Math.Min(S(700), area.Height - S(32));
        MinimumSize = new Size(Math.Min(S(560), area.Width), Math.Min(S(460), area.Height));
        Size = new Size(Math.Max(MinimumSize.Width, width), Math.Max(MinimumSize.Height, height));
        var center = ownerBounds.Width > 0 ? new Point(ownerBounds.Left + ownerBounds.Width / 2, ownerBounds.Top + ownerBounds.Height / 2) : new Point(area.Left + area.Width / 2, area.Top + area.Height / 2);
        Location = new Point(Math.Max(area.Left, Math.Min(center.X - Width / 2, area.Right - Width)), Math.Max(area.Top, Math.Min(center.Y - Height / 2, area.Bottom - Height)));
        ApplyLayout();

        bool disabled = false;
        if (owner.Handle != IntPtr.Zero && NativeWindows.IsWindowEnabled(owner.Handle)) { NativeWindows.EnableWindow(owner.Handle, false); disabled = true; }
        try
        {
            Show(owner); Activate(); exportButton.Focus();
            while (!finished && !IsDisposed)
            {
                Application.DoEvents();
                if (previewWanted && !finished) { previewWanted = false; Preview(generatePreview); continue; }
                if (!finished) NativeWindows.WaitMessage();
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            // Re-enable Revit before hiding so Windows returns focus to it.
            if (disabled) NativeWindows.EnableWindow(owner.Handle, true);
            if (!IsDisposed) Hide();
        }
        return Outcome;
    }

    private void Preview(Func<PreviewRequest, Action<string>, string> generatePreview)
    {
        var request = CurrentRequest();
        if (request.PixelSize.Width < 50 || request.PixelSize.Height < 50) request.PixelSize = new Size(900, 600);
        SetBusy(true); staleBanner.Visible = false;
        ShowOverlay("Generating preview…", BusyText(), null, busyState: true);
        status.Text = "Rendering the preview in Revit…";
        Refresh();
        try
        {
            string path = generatePreview(request, message =>
            {
                status.Text = message; overlayText.Text = message; Refresh(); Application.DoEvents();
                if (cancelAfterPreview) throw new OperationCanceledException("Preview cancelled");
            });
            LoadImage(path);
            shownKey = request.Key; overlay.Visible = false;
            status.Text = "Preview ready. It shows the scope selected when it was generated.";
            previewButton.Text = "Update preview";
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            ShowOverlay("Preview cancelled", "You can still export without a preview.", "Generate preview", busyState: false);
            status.Text = "";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // The caller has logged the details; the dialog stays usable.
            ShowOverlay("Preview could not be generated", ex.Message + "\n\nYou can still export without a preview.", "Try again", busyState: false);
            status.Text = "Preview failed; details are in the export log.";
        }
        finally
        {
            SetBusy(false);
            OptionsChanged();
            if (cancelAfterPreview) { Outcome = OptionsOutcome.Cancel; finished = true; }
        }
    }

    private void LoadImage(string path)
    {
        if (!File.Exists(path)) throw new FileNotFoundException("The generated preview image is missing.", path);
        // Clone before closing the stream so the temporary file is not locked.
        using (var stream = File.OpenRead(path))
        using (var image = Image.FromStream(stream))
        {
            var bitmap = new Bitmap(image); bitmap.SetResolution(96, 96);
            var old = picture.Image; picture.Image = bitmap; old?.Dispose();
            PreviewDiagnostics = "Loaded: " + path + " | Device DPI: " + DeviceDpi + " | control: " + picture.ClientSize.Width + "x" + picture.ClientSize.Height + " | PNG pixels: " + image.Width + "x" + image.Height;
        }
    }

    protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
    {
        if (disposing) { ticker.Dispose(); var image = picture.Image; picture.Image = null; image?.Dispose(); }
        base.Dispose(disposing);
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportProgressDialog.cs =====
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Form = System.Windows.Forms.Form;
using Button = System.Windows.Forms.Button;
using Label = System.Windows.Forms.Label;
using Timer = System.Windows.Forms.Timer;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

internal sealed class ExportProgressCounts
{
    public long Elements { get; set; }
    public long PlacedTriangles { get; set; }
    public long Reused { get; set; }
    public string? Current { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>Revit work stays on its API/UI thread. The caller pumps this form
/// between bounded chunks; no background thread ever calls the Revit API.
/// While it is shown, Revit's main window is disabled so no other command or
/// model edit can re-enter during the temporary transactions.</summary>
internal sealed class ExportProgressDialog : Form
{
    private readonly Label phaseLabel = new Label();
    private readonly Label counts = new Label();
    private readonly Label current = new Label();
    private readonly Label elapsedLabel = new Label();
    private readonly Button cancel = new Button();
    private readonly Stopwatch total = Stopwatch.StartNew();
    private readonly List<string> timing = new List<string>();
    private readonly Timer timer = new Timer { Interval = 250 };
    private string phase = "Preparing export";
    private long phaseStart, lastPump = -1000;
    private IntPtr disabledOwner;
    private bool completed, ownerRestored;
    public bool CancelRequested { get; private set; }
    public TimeSpan Elapsed => total.Elapsed;
    public IReadOnlyList<string> TimingLog => timing.Concat(new[] { Stamp(phase, total.ElapsedMilliseconds - phaseStart) }).ToArray();

    public ExportProgressDialog(string title = "Export 3D PDF")
    {
        SuspendLayout();
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None; // sizes are computed from DeviceDpi below
        Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f); Text = title; BackColor = Color.White;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog; StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
        ShowInTaskbar = false; MinimizeBox = false; MaximizeBox = false; ControlBox = true;
        int S(int px) => (int)Math.Round(px * DeviceDpi / 96.0);
        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, RowCount = 6, Padding = new Padding(S(18), S(16), S(18), S(14)) };
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100));
        for (int row = 0; row < 6; row++) layout.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        foreach (var label in new[] { phaseLabel, counts, current, elapsedLabel }) { label.AutoSize = true; label.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Right; label.Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, S(6)); }
        phaseLabel.Text = phase; phaseLabel.Font = new Font(Font.FontFamily, 10.5f, FontStyle.Bold);
        current.ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(90, 90, 90); current.AutoEllipsis = true; current.AutoSize = false; current.Height = S(20);
        elapsedLabel.ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(90, 90, 90);
        layout.Controls.Add(phaseLabel, 0, 0); layout.Controls.Add(counts, 0, 1); layout.Controls.Add(current, 0, 2);
        var bar = new ProgressBar { Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Right, Height = S(10), Margin = new Padding(0, S(4), 0, S(8)), Style = ProgressBarStyle.Marquee, MarqueeAnimationSpeed = 30 };
        layout.Controls.Add(bar, 0, 3); layout.Controls.Add(elapsedLabel, 0, 4);
        var footer = new TableLayoutPanel { Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Right, AutoSize = true, ColumnCount = 2, Margin = new Padding(0, S(8), 0, 0) };
        footer.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100)); footer.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        var note = new Label { AutoSize = true, Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Right, Text = "Some Revit steps must finish before Cancel takes effect.", ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(110, 110, 110), Margin = new Padding(0, S(6), S(12), 0) };
        cancel.AutoSize = true; cancel.MinimumSize = new Size(S(96), S(30)); cancel.Text = "Cancel"; cancel.Click += (_, __) => RequestCancel(); CancelButton = cancel;
        footer.Controls.Add(note, 0, 0); footer.Controls.Add(cancel, 1, 0);
        layout.Controls.Add(footer, 0, 5);
        Controls.Add(layout);
        ClientSize = new Size(S(520), S(220));
        ResumeLayout(true);
        timer.Tick += (_, __) => UpdateElapsed(); timer.Start(); UpdateElapsed();
    }

    public new void Show(IWin32Window owner)
    {
        CenterOn(owner.Handle);
        base.Show(owner);
        // DoEvents is needed to process Cancel while CustomExporter calls back.
        if (owner.Handle != IntPtr.Zero && NativeWindows.IsWindowEnabled(owner.Handle)) { disabledOwner = owner.Handle; NativeWindows.EnableWindow(disabledOwner, false); ownerRestored = false; }
        BringToFront(); Pump(phase);
    }

    private void CenterOn(IntPtr owner)
    {
        var area = NativeWindows.WorkingArea(owner, out var ownerBounds);
        int x = ownerBounds.Width > 0 ? ownerBounds.Left + (ownerBounds.Width - Width) / 2 : area.Left + (area.Width - Width) / 2;
        int y = ownerBounds.Height > 0 ? ownerBounds.Top + (ownerBounds.Height - Height) / 2 : area.Top + (area.Height - Height) / 2;
        Location = new Point(Math.Max(area.Left, Math.Min(x, area.Right - Width)), Math.Max(area.Top, Math.Min(y, area.Bottom - Height)));
    }

    public bool Pump(string stage, ExportProgressCounts? values = null)
    {
        if (IsDisposed || CancelRequested) return false;
        long now = total.ElapsedMilliseconds;
        bool changed = !string.Equals(phase, stage, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        if (changed) { timing.Add(Stamp(phase, now - phaseStart)); phase = stage; phaseStart = now; }
        if (changed || now - lastPump >= 100)
        {
            if (!CancelRequested) phaseLabel.Text = phase;
            counts.Text = values == null ? "" : values.Elements.ToString("N0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + " elements · " + values.PlacedTriangles.ToString("N0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + " triangles" + (values.Reused > 0 ? " · " + values.Reused.ToString("N0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + " repeated items reused" : "");
            current.Text = values?.Current ?? "";
            UpdateElapsed(); lastPump = now;
            if (Visible) { Refresh(); Application.DoEvents(); }
        }
        return !CancelRequested && !IsDisposed;
    }

    public void Complete() { completed = true; RestoreOwner(); Close(); }
    public new void Hide() { RestoreOwner(); base.Hide(); }
    private void RequestCancel() { CancelRequested = true; cancel.Enabled = false; cancel.Text = "Cancelling…"; phaseLabel.Text = "Cancelling at the next safe point…"; }
    private void UpdateElapsed() { elapsedLabel.Text = "Elapsed " + total.Elapsed.ToString(@"hh\:mm\:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture); }
    private static string Stamp(string phase, long milliseconds) => phase + " | " + milliseconds + " ms";
    private void RestoreOwner()
    {
        if (ownerRestored) return; ownerRestored = true;
        if (disabledOwner != IntPtr.Zero) { NativeWindows.EnableWindow(disabledOwner, true); disabledOwner = IntPtr.Zero; }
    }
    protected override void OnFormClosing(FormClosingEventArgs e) { if (!completed && !CancelRequested) { RequestCancel(); e.Cancel = true; } base.OnFormClosing(e); }
    protected override void OnFormClosed(FormClosedEventArgs e) { RestoreOwner(); base.OnFormClosed(e); }
    protected override void Dispose(bool disposing) { if (disposing) { timer.Stop(); timer.Dispose(); RestoreOwner(); } base.Dispose(disposing); }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportRegions.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;
namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

internal sealed class ExportRegion
{
    public Autodesk.Revit.DB.View Source {get;private set;}=null!;
    public Viewport Viewport {get;private set;}=null!;
    public BoundingBoxXYZ Box {get;private set;}=null!;
    public CropClipper BroadPhase {get;private set;}=null!;
    public ExportVolume Volume {get;private set;}=null!;
    public Point3 Min=>BroadPhase.Min;
    public Point3 Max=>BroadPhase.Max;
    public string TopLabel {get;private set;}="";
    public string BottomLabel {get;private set;}="";
    public ViewRegion Mapping {get;private set;}=null!;
    private BoundingBoxXYZ originalBox=null!;
    private Matrix4 graphics=null!;
    public static ExportRegion Create(Document doc,Autodesk.Revit.DB.View source,Viewport viewport,Matrix4 graphics)
    {
        BoundingBoxXYZ box;string top="Crop depth",bottom="Crop depth";double level=0;
        if(source is ViewPlan plan){
            var loops=source.GetCropRegionShapeManager().GetCropShape();
            if(loops.Count!=1||loops[0].Count()!=4||loops[0].Any(c=>!(c is Line)))throw new ExportSetupException("Use one rectangular unsplit crop in "+source.Name+".");
            var corners=loops[0].Select(c=>c.GetEndPoint(0)).ToArray();var s=ExportScope.From(doc,plan,corners);box=s.SectionBox;top=s.TopLabel;bottom=s.BottomLabel;level=s.LevelFeet;
        }else if(source is View3D three){
            box=three.IsSectionBoxActive?three.GetSectionBox():VisibleBox(doc,source);top="3D view bounds";bottom=top;
        }else box=source.CropBox;
        // Use the actual view crop and adjacent levels, without expanding it
        // into extra model area. Keep the oriented frame for rotated crops.
        var points=BoxPoints(box).ToArray();var min=new Point3(points.Min(p=>p.X),points.Min(p=>p.Y),points.Min(p=>p.Z));var max=new Point3(points.Max(p=>p.X),points.Max(p=>p.Y),points.Max(p=>p.Z));
        var polygon=new[]{new Point3(min.X,min.Y),new Point3(max.X,min.Y),new Point3(max.X,max.Y),new Point3(min.X,max.Y)};
        var transforms=source.GetModelToProjectionTransforms();if(transforms.Count!=1)throw new ExportSetupException("Split projection regions are not supported: "+source.Name);
        var toSheet=Adapter.Matrix(viewport.GetProjectionToSheetTransform())*Adapter.Matrix(transforms[0].GetModelToProjectionTransform());
        var outline=viewport.GetBoxOutline();var a=outline.MinimumPoint;var b=outline.MaximumPoint;
        var center=new Point3((min.X+max.X)/2,(min.Y+max.Y)/2,(min.Z+max.Z)/2);
        var origin=source is ViewPlan?new Point3(center.X,center.Y,level):center;
        var normal=source is ViewPlan?new Point3(0,0,1):Adapter.Point(source.ViewDirection);
        var gp=points.Select(p=>graphics.Apply(Adapter.Point(p))).ToArray();
        return new ExportRegion{Source=source,Viewport=viewport,Box=box,originalBox=box,graphics=graphics,Volume=new ExportVolume(Adapter.Point(box.Min),Adapter.Point(box.Max),Adapter.Matrix(box.Transform)),BroadPhase=new CropClipper(polygon,min.Z,max.Z),TopLabel=top,BottomLabel=bottom,
            Mapping=new ViewRegion{ViewId=source.UniqueId,Name=source.Name,Kind=source.ViewType.ToString(),ModelToSheet=toSheet,CropSheet=new[]{a.X,a.Y,b.X,a.Y,b.X,b.Y,a.X,b.Y},PlaneOrigin=new[]{origin.X,origin.Y,origin.Z},PlaneNormal=new[]{normal.X,normal.Y,normal.Z},LevelFeet=source is ViewPlan?level:center.Z,BoundsGraphics=new[]{gp.Min(p=>p.X),gp.Min(p=>p.Y),gp.Min(p=>p.Z),gp.Max(p=>p.X),gp.Max(p=>p.Y),gp.Max(p=>p.Z)}}};
    }
    public void ConfigureBounds(bool outside,bool allUp,bool allDown,BoundingBoxXYZ? modelBounds)
    {
        var lo=originalBox.Min;var hi=originalBox.Max;
        if(modelBounds!=null&&(outside||allUp||allDown)){
            var inverse=originalBox.Transform.Inverse;var p=BoxPoints(modelBounds).Select(inverse.OfPoint).ToArray();
            if(Source is ViewPlan){var bounds=ExportVolume.PlanBounds(Adapter.Point(lo),Adapter.Point(hi),new Point3(p.Min(v=>v.X),p.Min(v=>v.Y),p.Min(v=>v.Z)),new Point3(p.Max(v=>v.X),p.Max(v=>v.Y),p.Max(v=>v.Z)),outside,allUp,allDown);lo=new XYZ(bounds[0].X,bounds[0].Y,bounds[0].Z);hi=new XYZ(bounds[1].X,bounds[1].Y,bounds[1].Z);}
            else if(outside){lo=new XYZ(p.Min(v=>v.X),p.Min(v=>v.Y),p.Min(v=>v.Z));hi=new XYZ(p.Max(v=>v.X),p.Max(v=>v.Y),p.Max(v=>v.Z));}
        }
        Box=new BoundingBoxXYZ{Transform=originalBox.Transform,Min=lo,Max=hi};Volume=new ExportVolume(Adapter.Point(lo),Adapter.Point(hi),Adapter.Matrix(Box.Transform));
        var host=BoxPoints(Box).Select(Adapter.Point).ToArray();var min=new Point3(host.Min(p=>p.X),host.Min(p=>p.Y),host.Min(p=>p.Z));var max=new Point3(host.Max(p=>p.X),host.Max(p=>p.Y),host.Max(p=>p.Z));BroadPhase=new CropClipper(new[]{new Point3(min.X,min.Y),new Point3(max.X,min.Y),new Point3(max.X,max.Y),new Point3(min.X,max.Y)},min.Z,max.Z);
        var gp=host.Select(graphics.Apply).ToArray();Mapping.BoundsGraphics=new[]{gp.Min(p=>p.X),gp.Min(p=>p.Y),gp.Min(p=>p.Z),gp.Max(p=>p.X),gp.Max(p=>p.Y),gp.Max(p=>p.Z)};
    }
    public bool Intersects(BoundingBoxXYZ? elementBox,Matrix4 frame)
    {
        if(elementBox==null)return true;var points=BoxPoints(elementBox).Select(p=>frame.Apply(Adapter.Point(p))).ToArray();
        if(!BroadPhase.IntersectsBounds(new Point3(points.Min(p=>p.X),points.Min(p=>p.Y),points.Min(p=>p.Z)),new Point3(points.Max(p=>p.X),points.Max(p=>p.Y),points.Max(p=>p.Z))))return false;
        var inverse=Adapter.Matrix(Box.Transform.Inverse);var local=points.Select(inverse.Apply).ToArray();return local.Max(p=>p.X)>=Box.Min.X&&local.Min(p=>p.X)<=Box.Max.X&&local.Max(p=>p.Y)>=Box.Min.Y&&local.Min(p=>p.Y)<=Box.Max.Y&&local.Max(p=>p.Z)>=Box.Min.Z&&local.Min(p=>p.Z)<=Box.Max.Z;
    }
    public ICollection<ElementId> Candidates(Document doc)=>new FilteredElementCollector(doc).WhereElementIsNotElementType().WherePasses(new BoundingBoxIntersectsFilter(new Outline(new XYZ(Min.X,Min.Y,Min.Z),new XYZ(Max.X,Max.Y,Max.Z)))).ToElementIds();
    internal static IEnumerable<XYZ> BoxPoints(BoundingBoxXYZ box){for(int i=0;i<8;i++)yield return box.Transform.OfPoint(new XYZ((i&1)==0?box.Min.X:box.Max.X,(i&2)==0?box.Min.Y:box.Max.Y,(i&4)==0?box.Min.Z:box.Max.Z));}
    private static BoundingBoxXYZ VisibleBox(Document doc,Autodesk.Revit.DB.View view){
        var points=new FilteredElementCollector(doc,view.Id).WhereElementIsNotElementType().Select(e=>e.get_BoundingBox(view)).Where(b=>b!=null).SelectMany(b=>BoxPoints(b!)).ToArray();
        if(points.Length==0)throw new ExportSetupException("No visible model bounds in "+view.Name);
        return new BoundingBoxXYZ{Min=new XYZ(points.Min(p=>p.X),points.Min(p=>p.Y),points.Min(p=>p.Z)),Max=new XYZ(points.Max(p=>p.X),points.Max(p=>p.Y),points.Max(p=>p.Z))};
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportScope.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>One crop extrusion between nearest distinct project levels.</summary>
internal sealed class ExportScope
{
    public double LevelFeet{get;private set;}
    public double MinZ{get;private set;}
    public double MaxZ{get;private set;}
    public double AboveFeet=>MaxZ-LevelFeet;
    public double BelowFeet=>LevelFeet-MinZ;
    public string TopLabel{get;private set;}="";
    public string BottomLabel{get;private set;}="";
    public bool TopFallback{get;private set;}
    public bool BottomFallback{get;private set;}
    public BoundingBoxXYZ SectionBox{get;private set;}=null!;
    public CropClipper Clipper{get;private set;}=null!;

    public static ExportScope From(Document doc,ViewPlan plan,XYZ[] corners,double fallbackAboveFeet=10,double fallbackBelowFeet=10)
    {
        if(plan.GenLevel==null)throw new ExportSetupException("A floor plan with a known level is required.");
        if(corners==null||corners.Length<3)throw new ExportSetupException("The crop boundary is missing.");
        var levels=new FilteredElementCollector(doc).OfClass(typeof(Level)).Cast<Level>().Select(l=>new LevelDatum(l.Name,l.ProjectElevation));
        var range=AdjacentLevelRange.Select(plan.GenLevel.ProjectElevation,levels,fallbackAboveFeet,fallbackBelowFeet);
        var polygon=corners.Select(p=>new Point3(p.X,p.Y,p.Z)).ToArray();var clipper=new CropClipper(polygon,range.MinZ,range.MaxZ);
        var first=corners[1]-corners[0];var x=new XYZ(first.X,first.Y,0).Normalize();var y=XYZ.BasisZ.CrossProduct(x);
        var origin=new XYZ(corners.Average(p=>p.X),corners.Average(p=>p.Y),0);
        var frame=Transform.Identity;frame.Origin=origin;frame.BasisX=x;frame.BasisY=y;frame.BasisZ=XYZ.BasisZ;
        var inverse=frame.Inverse;var local=corners.Select(inverse.OfPoint).ToArray();
        var section=new BoundingBoxXYZ{Transform=frame,Min=new XYZ(local.Min(p=>p.X),local.Min(p=>p.Y),range.MinZ),Max=new XYZ(local.Max(p=>p.X),local.Max(p=>p.Y),range.MaxZ)};
        return new ExportScope{LevelFeet=range.LevelFeet,MinZ=range.MinZ,MaxZ=range.MaxZ,TopLabel=range.TopLabel,BottomLabel=range.BottomLabel,TopFallback=range.TopFallback,BottomFallback=range.BottomFallback,SectionBox=section,Clipper=clipper};
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportTarget.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;
using Autodesk.Revit.UI;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Form=System.Windows.Forms.Form;
using ComboBox=System.Windows.Forms.ComboBox;
using Button=System.Windows.Forms.Button;
using Control=System.Windows.Forms.Control;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

internal sealed class ExportSetupException:Exception
{
    public ExportSetupException(string message):base(message){}
}

/// <summary>Resolves one plan without requiring an on-canvas selection. All
/// direct-view preparation is temporary and is rolled back on every exit.</summary>
internal sealed class ExportTarget:IDisposable
{
    private TransactionGroup? temporary;
    public ViewSheet Sheet{get;private set;}=null!;
    public Viewport Viewport{get;private set;}=null!;
    public Autodesk.Revit.DB.View Plan{get;private set;}=null!;
    public Autodesk.Revit.DB.View SourcePlan{get;private set;}=null!;
    public Viewport[] Viewports {get;private set;}=Array.Empty<Viewport>();
    public bool FromPlan{get;private set;}
    public string FileStem=>FromPlan?SourcePlan.Name:Sheet.SheetNumber;

    public static bool IsFloorPlan(Autodesk.Revit.DB.View? view)=>view!=null&&!view.IsTemplate&&(view.ViewType==ViewType.FloorPlan||view.ViewType==ViewType.CeilingPlan);
    public static bool IsSupportedView(Autodesk.Revit.DB.View? view)=>view!=null&&!view.IsTemplate&&view.HasViewTransforms()&&(IsFloorPlan(view)||view.ViewType==ViewType.Section||view.ViewType==ViewType.Elevation||(view is View3D v&&!v.IsPerspective));

    public static ExportTarget? Create(UIDocument ui,IntPtr owner)
    {
        var result=new ExportTarget();
        try{
            var doc=ui.Document;
            if(IsSupportedView(doc.ActiveView)){
                result.SourcePlan=doc.ActiveView;
                var duplicate=result.SourcePlan.CanViewBeDuplicated(ViewDuplicateOption.WithDetailing)?ViewDuplicateOption.WithDetailing:ViewDuplicateOption.Duplicate;
                if(!result.SourcePlan.CanViewBeDuplicated(duplicate))
                    throw new ExportSetupException("This view cannot be copied for export. Open an independent supported model view and try again.");
                result.FromPlan=true;
                result.temporary=new TransactionGroup(doc,"Offline3D temporary plan export");
                result.temporary.Start();
                using(var tx=new Autodesk.Revit.DB.Transaction(doc,"Prepare temporary PDF page")){
                    tx.Start();
                    result.Plan=(Autodesk.Revit.DB.View)doc.GetElement(result.SourcePlan.Duplicate(duplicate));
                    result.Plan.Name="Offline3D_TEMP_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
                    // Detaching only the temporary copy permits a crop controlled
                    // by a template to be enabled without editing the user's view.
                    result.Plan.ViewTemplateId=ElementId.InvalidElementId;
                    if(!(result.Plan is View3D))result.Plan.CropBoxActive=true;
                    result.Plan.CropBoxVisible=false;
                    result.Sheet=ViewSheet.Create(doc,ElementId.InvalidElementId);
                    result.Sheet.Name="Offline 3D PDF temporary page";
                    if(!Autodesk.Revit.DB.Viewport.CanAddViewToSheet(doc,result.Sheet.Id,result.Plan.Id))
                        throw new ExportSetupException("Revit could not prepare a PDF page for this view. Open an independent supported model view and try again.");
                    result.Viewport=Autodesk.Revit.DB.Viewport.Create(doc,result.Sheet.Id,result.Plan.Id,new XYZ(0,0,0));
                    var viewportType=(ElementType)doc.GetElement(result.Viewport.GetTypeId());
                    var exportType=viewportType.Duplicate("Offline3D_TEMP_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                    var showLabel=exportType.get_Parameter(BuiltInParameter.VIEWPORT_ATTR_SHOW_LABEL);
                    if(showLabel!=null&&!showLabel.IsReadOnly)showLabel.Set(0);
                    result.Viewport.ChangeTypeId(exportType.Id);
                    tx.Commit();
                }
            }else if(doc.ActiveView is ViewSheet sheet){
                result.Sheet=sheet;
                var all=sheet.GetAllViewports().Select(id=>(Viewport)doc.GetElement(id)).ToArray();
                var plans=all.Where(v=>IsSupportedView(doc.GetElement(v.ViewId) as Autodesk.Revit.DB.View)).ToArray();
                if(plans.Length==0)throw new ExportSetupException("This sheet has no supported model viewport. Open a plan, RCP, section, elevation or orthographic 3D view.");
                int choice=AutomaticPlanChoice.SelectIndex(plans.Select(v=>v.Id).ToArray(),ui.Selection.GetElementIds());
                result.Viewport=plans[Math.Max(0,choice)];
                result.Viewports=new[]{result.Viewport}.Concat(plans.Where(v=>v.Id!=result.Viewport.Id)).ToArray();
                result.Plan=(Autodesk.Revit.DB.View)doc.GetElement(result.Viewport.ViewId);result.SourcePlan=result.Plan;
            }else throw new ExportSetupException("Open a plan, RCP, section, elevation, orthographic 3D view, or a sheet containing one, then click Export 3D PDF.");
            if(result.Viewports.Length==0)result.Viewports=new[]{result.Viewport};
            return result;
        }catch{result.Dispose();throw;}
    }

    private static SheetBounds Bounds(Viewport viewport)
    {
        var box=viewport.GetBoxOutline();var title=viewport.GetLabelOutline();
        if(title.IsEmpty)return new SheetBounds(box.MinimumPoint.X,box.MinimumPoint.Y,box.MaximumPoint.X,box.MaximumPoint.Y);
        return new SheetBounds(Math.Min(box.MinimumPoint.X,title.MinimumPoint.X),Math.Min(box.MinimumPoint.Y,title.MinimumPoint.Y),Math.Max(box.MaximumPoint.X,title.MaximumPoint.X),Math.Max(box.MaximumPoint.Y,title.MaximumPoint.Y));
    }

    public void Restore()
    {
        if(temporary==null)return;
        try{if(temporary.GetStatus()==TransactionStatus.Started)temporary.RollBack();}
        finally{temporary.Dispose();temporary=null;}
    }
    public void Dispose()=>Restore();
}

internal sealed class RevitWindow:IWin32Window
{
    public IntPtr Handle{get;}
    public RevitWindow(IntPtr handle){Handle=handle;}
}

internal sealed class PlanPicker:Form
{
    private readonly ComboBox plans;
    public int SelectedIndex=>plans.SelectedIndex;
    public PlanPicker(string[] names)
    {
        Text="Export 3D PDF";Width=560;Height=205;FormBorderStyle=FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        StartPosition=FormStartPosition.CenterParent;MinimizeBox=false;MaximizeBox=false;ShowInTaskbar=false;
        var label=new Label{Text="Choose the floor plan for clickable 3D on this PDF page:",Left=18,Top=18,Width=500,Height=24};
        plans=new ComboBox{Left=18,Top=50,Width=500,DropDownStyle=ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList};
        plans.Items.AddRange(names);plans.SelectedIndex=0;
        var export=new Button{Text="Continue",Left=308,Top=100,Width=100,DialogResult=DialogResult.OK};
        var cancel=new Button{Text="Cancel",Left=418,Top=100,Width=100,DialogResult=DialogResult.Cancel};
        Controls.AddRange(new Control[]{label,plans,export,cancel});AcceptButton=export;CancelButton=cancel;
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportTrace.cs =====
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>
/// Live, flushed export log. One copy lives in the export work folder (the
/// viewer helper deletes that folder after success); the other stays under
/// %LOCALAPPDATA%\Offline3DPdfPrototype\Logs\Exports, so a slow, cancelled,
/// failed or crashed export always leaves a readable trace.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class ExportTrace : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Stopwatch clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
    private readonly List<StreamWriter> writers = new List<StreamWriter>();
    private long lastHeartbeat = -1;
    private string stage = "Starting";
    private long stageStart;
    private readonly List<string> stages = new List<string>();

    public string? LogDirectory { get; }

    public ExportTrace(string workFolder)
    {
        Open(Path.Combine(workFolder, "export-trace.txt"));
        try
        {
            string logs = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "Offline3DPdfPrototype", "Logs", "Exports", DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "-revit-" + Path.GetFileName(workFolder));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(logs);
            Open(Path.Combine(logs, "export-trace.txt"));
            LogDirectory = logs;
        }
        catch (IOException) { }
        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
    }

    private void Open(string path)
    {
        try { writers.Add(new StreamWriter(new FileStream(path, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite | FileShare.Delete), new UTF8Encoding(false)) { AutoFlush = true }); }
        catch (IOException) { }
        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
    }

    public TimeSpan Elapsed => clock.Elapsed;

    public void Line(string text)
    {
        string line = "[" + clock.Elapsed.ToString(@"hh\:mm\:ss\.fff", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "] " + text;
        foreach (var writer in writers) { try { writer.WriteLine(line); } catch (IOException) { } catch (ObjectDisposedException) { } }
    }

    public void Lines(IEnumerable<string> lines) { foreach (var line in lines) Line(line); }

    /// <summary>Marks the start of a stage and logs how long the previous one took.</summary>
    public void Stage(string name)
    {
        long now = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
        string done = stage + ": " + (now - stageStart).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " ms";
        stages.Add(done);
        Line("Stage done: " + done + " | next: " + name + " | " + Memory());
        stage = name; stageStart = now;
    }

    public IReadOnlyList<string> StageTimes => stages.Concat(new[] { stage + ": " + (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - stageStart).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " ms (current)" }).ToList();

    /// <summary>At most one line every few seconds; the last one shows what
    /// Revit was working on if it stops responding.</summary>
    public void Heartbeat(Func<string> snapshot, int intervalMs = 5000)
    {
        long now = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
        if (lastHeartbeat >= 0 && now - lastHeartbeat < intervalMs) return;
        lastHeartbeat = now;
        Line("Progress: " + snapshot() + " | " + Memory());
    }

    public static string Memory()
    {
        try
        {
            using var process = Process.GetCurrentProcess();
            return "private " + (process.PrivateMemorySize64 >> 20) + " MiB, peak working set " + (process.PeakWorkingSet64 >> 20) + " MiB, managed " + (GC.GetTotalMemory(false) >> 20) + " MiB";
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException) { return "memory unavailable"; }
    }

    /// <summary>Copies a diagnostics file into the persistent log folder.</summary>
    public void Keep(string file)
    {
        if (LogDirectory == null || !File.Exists(file)) return;
        try { File.Copy(file, Path.Combine(LogDirectory, Path.GetFileName(file)), true); } catch (IOException) { } catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        foreach (var writer in writers) { try { writer.Dispose(); } catch (IOException) { } }
        writers.Clear();
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/ExportWireJson.cs =====
using System.Collections;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;

namespace Offline3D;

/// <summary>Writes only trusted exporter DTOs. Revit never reads JSON here;
/// parsing and full contract/GLB validation stay in the self-contained helper.
/// No NuGet JSON package or shared Core assembly is loaded into the Revit host.</summary>
public static class Contract
{
    public const int MaxResource=64*1024*1024;
    private const int MaxDepth=32;
    private static readonly Dictionary<Type,PropertyInfo[]> Properties=new Dictionary<Type,PropertyInfo[]>();
    private static readonly object PropertyGate=new object();

    public static byte[] Encode<T>(T value)=>EncodeValue(value,true);
    public static byte[] EncodeExact<T>(T value)=>EncodeValue(value,false);
    public static string Hash(byte[] bytes)
    {
        if(bytes==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(bytes));
        using(var sha=SHA256.Create())return BitConverter.ToString(sha.ComputeHash(bytes)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();
    }

    private static byte[] EncodeValue(object? value,bool camelCase)
    {
        using(var output=new BoundedOutput()){
            using(var writer=new StreamWriter(output,new UTF8Encoding(false,true),4096,true)){
                WriteValue(writer,value,camelCase,0,new HashSet<object>(ReferenceComparer.Instance));
                writer.Flush();
            }
            return output.ToArray();
        }
    }

    private static void WriteValue(TextWriter writer,object? value,bool camelCase,int depth,HashSet<object> parents)
    {
        if(value==null){writer.Write("null");return;}
        if(value is string text){WriteString(writer,text);return;}
        if(value is char character){WriteString(writer,character.ToString());return;}
        if(value is bool boolean){writer.Write(boolean?"true":"false");return;}
        if(value is double number){if(double.IsNaN(number)||double.IsInfinity(number))throw new InvalidDataException("Non-finite JSON number");writer.Write(number.ToString("R",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));return;}
        if(value is float single){if(float.IsNaN(single)||float.IsInfinity(single))throw new InvalidDataException("Non-finite JSON number");writer.Write(single.ToString("R",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));return;}
        if(value is decimal dec){writer.Write(dec.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));return;}
        if(value is byte||value is sbyte||value is short||value is ushort||value is int||value is uint||value is long||value is ulong){writer.Write(((IFormattable)value).ToString(null,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));return;}
        var type=value.GetType();
        if(type.IsEnum){WriteValue(writer,Convert.ChangeType(value,Enum.GetUnderlyingType(type),CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),camelCase,depth,parents);return;}
        if(depth>=MaxDepth)throw new InvalidDataException("JSON exceeds maximum depth");
        bool tracked=!type.IsValueType;
        if(tracked&&!parents.Add(value))throw new InvalidDataException("Cyclic exporter data");
        try{
            if(value is IDictionary dictionary){
                writer.Write('{');bool first=true;
                foreach(DictionaryEntry entry in dictionary){
                    if(!(entry.Key is string key))throw new InvalidDataException("Only string JSON dictionary keys are supported");
                    if(!first)writer.Write(',');first=false;WriteString(writer,key);writer.Write(':');WriteValue(writer,entry.Value,camelCase,depth+1,parents);
                }
                writer.Write('}');return;
            }
            if(value is IEnumerable sequence){
                writer.Write('[');bool first=true;
                foreach(var item in sequence){if(!first)writer.Write(',');first=false;WriteValue(writer,item,camelCase,depth+1,parents);}
                writer.Write(']');return;
            }
            bool anonymous=type.IsDefined(typeof(CompilerGeneratedAttribute),false)&&type.Name.IndexOf("AnonymousType",StringComparison.Ordinal)>=0;
            if(type.Namespace!="Offline3D"&&!anonymous)throw new InvalidDataException("Unsupported exporter JSON type: "+type.FullName);
            PropertyInfo[] properties;
            lock(PropertyGate){if(!Properties.TryGetValue(type,out properties!)){
                properties=type.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Instance|BindingFlags.Public).Where(p=>p.CanRead&&p.GetGetMethod(false)!=null&&p.GetIndexParameters().Length==0).OrderBy(p=>p.MetadataToken).ToArray();
                Properties.Add(type,properties);
            }}
            writer.Write('{');bool firstProperty=true;
            foreach(var property in properties){
                if(!firstProperty)writer.Write(',');firstProperty=false;
                WriteString(writer,camelCase?CamelCase(property.Name):property.Name);writer.Write(':');
                WriteValue(writer,property.GetValue(value,null),camelCase,depth+1,parents);
            }
            writer.Write('}');
        }finally{if(tracked)parents.Remove(value);}
    }

    private static string CamelCase(string name)
    {
        if(name.Length==0||!char.IsUpper(name[0]))return name;
        var chars=name.ToCharArray();
        for(int i=0;i<chars.Length;i++){
            if(i==1&&!char.IsUpper(chars[i]))break;
            if(i>0&&i+1<chars.Length&&!char.IsUpper(chars[i+1])){
                if(chars[i+1]==' ')chars[i]=char.ToLowerInvariant(chars[i]);
                break;
            }
            chars[i]=char.ToLowerInvariant(chars[i]);
        }
        return new string(chars);
    }

    private static void WriteString(TextWriter writer,string value)
    {
        writer.Write('"');
        for(int i=0;i<value.Length;i++){
            char c=value[i];
            if(char.IsHighSurrogate(c)){
                if(i+1>=value.Length||!char.IsLowSurrogate(value[i+1]))throw new InvalidDataException("Unpaired Unicode surrogate in exporter text");
                Escape(writer,c);Escape(writer,value[++i]);continue;
            }
            if(char.IsLowSurrogate(c))throw new InvalidDataException("Unpaired Unicode surrogate in exporter text");
            switch(c){
                case '"':writer.Write("\\\"");break;
                case '\\':writer.Write("\\\\");break;
                case '\b':writer.Write("\\b");break;
                case '\f':writer.Write("\\f");break;
                case '\n':writer.Write("\\n");break;
                case '\r':writer.Write("\\r");break;
                case '\t':writer.Write("\\t");break;
                default:if(c<' '||c>'~'||c=='<'||c=='>'||c=='&'||c=='\'')Escape(writer,c);else writer.Write(c);break;
            }
        }
        writer.Write('"');
    }
    private static void Escape(TextWriter writer,char value){writer.Write("\\u");writer.Write(((int)value).ToString("x4",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));}

    private sealed class ReferenceComparer:IEqualityComparer<object>
    {
        public static readonly ReferenceComparer Instance=new ReferenceComparer();
        public new bool Equals(object? x,object? y)=>ReferenceEquals(x,y);
        public int GetHashCode(object value)=>RuntimeHelpers.GetHashCode(value);
    }
    private sealed class BoundedOutput:Stream
    {
        private readonly MemoryStream inner=new MemoryStream();
        public override bool CanRead=>false;
        public override bool CanSeek=>false;
        public override bool CanWrite=>true;
        public override long Length=>inner.Length;
        public override long Position{get=>inner.Position;set=>throw new NotSupportedException();}
        public override void Flush()=>inner.Flush();
        public override void Write(byte[] buffer,int offset,int count){if(count<0||inner.Length+count>MaxResource)throw new InvalidDataException("Exporter JSON exceeds 64 MiB");inner.Write(buffer,offset,count);}
        public override void WriteByte(byte value){if(inner.Length>=MaxResource)throw new InvalidDataException("Exporter JSON exceeds 64 MiB");inner.WriteByte(value);}
        public byte[] ToArray()=>inner.ToArray();
        public override int Read(byte[] buffer,int offset,int count)=>throw new NotSupportedException();
        public override long Seek(long offset,SeekOrigin origin)=>throw new NotSupportedException();
        public override void SetLength(long value)=>throw new NotSupportedException();
        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing){if(disposing)inner.Dispose();base.Dispose(disposing);}
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/LinkOverrideTransfer.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>Link graphic settings are optional. Some supported model views do
/// not implement this API; retain the temporary view's By Host View rendering
/// instead of aborting geometry export. Element/link hides are copied separately.</summary>
internal static class LinkOverrideTransfer
{
    public static void Copy(Document doc,Autodesk.Revit.DB.View source,Autodesk.Revit.DB.View target,Action<string> warn)
    {
        if(!source.AreGraphicsOverridesAllowed()||!target.AreGraphicsOverridesAllowed()){
            warn("Link graphics overrides are unavailable in this view. Loaded visible links use By Host View rendering.");
            return;
        }
        bool canRead=true;
        foreach(var link in new FilteredElementCollector(doc).OfClass(typeof(RevitLinkInstance)).Cast<RevitLinkInstance>()){
            RevitLinkGraphicsSettings? settings=null;
            if(canRead){
                try{
                    // A null instance override inherits its link-type settings.
                    settings=source.GetLinkOverrides(link.Id)??source.GetLinkOverrides(link.GetTypeId());
                }catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.InvalidOperationException){
                    canRead=false;
                    warn("The source view does not support link graphical overrides. Loaded visible links use By Host View rendering.");
                }catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ArgumentException){
                    warn("A link graphics override could not be read. Using By Host View rendering for link "+link.Id+".");
                }
            }
            if(settings?.LinkVisibilityType==LinkVisibility.Custom){
                settings=null;
                warn("Custom link graphics cannot be transferred by this Revit API. Using By Host View rendering for link "+link.Id+".");
            }
            settings=settings??ByHost();
            try{target.SetLinkOverrides(link.Id,settings);}
            catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.InvalidOperationException){
                warn("The temporary 3D view does not support link graphical overrides. Its default By Host View rendering is retained.");
                return;
            }
            catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ArgumentException){
                // Linked-view settings can be valid in a plan yet invalid in 3D.
                // Reset explicitly so a previous region's settings cannot leak.
                warn("A source link graphics override is incompatible with the temporary 3D view. Using By Host View rendering for link "+link.Id+".");
                try{target.SetLinkOverrides(link.Id,ByHost());}
                catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.InvalidOperationException){
                    warn("The temporary 3D view does not support link graphical overrides. Its default rendering is retained.");
                    return;
                }
                catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ArgumentException){
                    warn("Link graphics could not be reset for link "+link.Id+". The temporary view's existing rendering is retained.");
                }
            }
        }
    }
    private static RevitLinkGraphicsSettings ByHost()=>new RevitLinkGraphicsSettings{LinkVisibilityType=LinkVisibility.ByHostView};
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/NativeWindows.cs =====
using System.Drawing;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>The few Win32 calls the exporter windows need.</summary>
internal static class NativeWindows
{
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] private struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }

    [DllImport("user32.dll")][return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] public static extern bool EnableWindow(IntPtr window, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] bool enabled);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")][return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] public static extern bool IsWindowEnabled(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")][return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] public static extern bool WaitMessage();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")][return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr window, out RECT rect);

    /// <summary>Working area of the monitor showing <paramref name="owner"/>, and the owner's bounds.</summary>
    public static Rectangle WorkingArea(IntPtr owner, out Rectangle ownerBounds)
    {
        ownerBounds = Rectangle.Empty;
        if (owner != IntPtr.Zero && GetWindowRect(owner, out var r) && r.Right > r.Left && r.Bottom > r.Top)
            ownerBounds = Rectangle.FromLTRB(r.Left, r.Top, r.Right, r.Bottom);
        return owner != IntPtr.Zero ? Screen.FromHandle(owner).WorkingArea : Screen.PrimaryScreen!.WorkingArea;
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/PreviewFraming.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;
namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;
internal static class PreviewFraming
{
    public static void Apply(Document doc,View3D view,BoundingBoxXYZ? region,System.Drawing.Size previewSize,Action<string> warn){
        view.AreAnnotationCategoriesHidden=true;
        foreach(var category in new[]{BuiltInCategory.OST_Levels,BuiltInCategory.OST_Grids,BuiltInCategory.OST_CLines,BuiltInCategory.OST_VolumeOfInterest,BuiltInCategory.OST_SectionBox}){var id=new ElementId(category);if(view.CanCategoryBeHidden(id))view.SetCategoryHidden(id,true);}
        // Preview-only: linked datums inherit these host category hides.
        foreach(var link in new FilteredElementCollector(doc).OfClass(typeof(RevitLinkInstance)).Cast<RevitLinkInstance>())try{view.SetLinkOverrides(link.Id,new RevitLinkGraphicsSettings{LinkVisibilityType=LinkVisibility.ByHostView});}catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.InvalidOperationException){}catch(Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ArgumentException){}
        doc.Regenerate();var bounds=region??ModelBounds(doc,view);if(bounds==null){warn("No model geometry bounds were found for preview framing.");return;}
        var crop=view.CropBox;var inverse=crop.Transform.Inverse;var corners=ExportRegion.BoxPoints(bounds).Select(inverse.OfPoint).ToArray();double minX=corners.Min(p=>p.X),maxX=corners.Max(p=>p.X),minY=corners.Min(p=>p.Y),maxY=corners.Max(p=>p.Y),z=corners.Min(p=>p.Z),Z=corners.Max(p=>p.Z);double aspect=previewSize.Height>0?(double)previewSize.Width/previewSize.Height:1.5,w=Math.Max(.01,maxX-minX)*1.12,h=Math.Max(.01,maxY-minY)*1.12;aspect=Math.Max(.2,Math.Min(5,aspect));w=Math.Max(w,h*aspect);h=Math.Max(h,w/aspect);double x=(minX+maxX)/2,y=(minY+maxY)/2;
        crop.Min=new XYZ(x-w/2,y-h/2,z-2);crop.Max=new XYZ(x+w/2,y+h/2,Z+2);view.CropBox=crop;view.CropBoxActive=true;view.CropBoxVisible=false;
    }
    internal static BoundingBoxXYZ? ModelBounds(Document doc,View3D view){
        XYZ? min=null,max=null;
        void Include(XYZ p){min=min==null?p:new XYZ(Math.Min(min.X,p.X),Math.Min(min.Y,p.Y),Math.Min(min.Z,p.Z));max=max==null?p:new XYZ(Math.Max(max.X,p.X),Math.Max(max.Y,p.Y),Math.Max(max.Z,p.Z));}
        var linkBounds=new Dictionary<Document,BoundingBoxXYZ?>();
        BoundingBoxXYZ? LinkBounds(Document linked){if(linkBounds.TryGetValue(linked,out var b))return b;linkBounds.Add(linked,null);XYZ? lo=null,hi=null;foreach(var e in new FilteredElementCollector(linked).WhereElementIsNotElementType()){if(e.Category?.CategoryType!=CategoryType.Model||e.ViewSpecific||e is DatumPlane||e is Group||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_VolumeOfInterest||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_SectionBox||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_CLines)continue;var nested=e as RevitLinkInstance;var nestedDoc=nested?.GetLinkDocument();var box=nested==null?e.get_BoundingBox(null):nestedDoc==null?null:LinkBounds(nestedDoc);if(box==null)continue;foreach(var raw in ExportRegion.BoxPoints(box)){var p=nested==null?raw:nested.GetTotalTransform().OfPoint(raw);lo=lo==null?p:new XYZ(Math.Min(lo.X,p.X),Math.Min(lo.Y,p.Y),Math.Min(lo.Z,p.Z));hi=hi==null?p:new XYZ(Math.Max(hi.X,p.X),Math.Max(hi.Y,p.Y),Math.Max(hi.Z,p.Z));}}b=lo==null?null:new BoundingBoxXYZ{Min=lo,Max=hi!};linkBounds[linked]=b;return b;}
        foreach(var e in new FilteredElementCollector(doc,view.Id).WhereElementIsNotElementType()){
            if(e.Category?.CategoryType!=CategoryType.Model||e.ViewSpecific||e is DatumPlane||e is Group||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_VolumeOfInterest||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_SectionBox||e.Category.Id.Value==(long)BuiltInCategory.OST_CLines)continue;
            if(e is RevitLinkInstance link){var linked=link.GetLinkDocument();if(linked==null)continue;var box=LinkBounds(linked);if(box==null)continue;foreach(var p in ExportRegion.BoxPoints(box))Include(link.GetTotalTransform().OfPoint(p));}
            else {var box=e.get_BoundingBox(view);if(box!=null)foreach(var p in ExportRegion.BoxPoints(box))Include(p);}
        }
        return min==null?null:new BoundingBoxXYZ{Min=min,Max=max!};
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/PreviewImageFiles.cs =====
namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>Each request gets a separate folder; never display an older request's image.</summary>
internal static class PreviewImageFiles
{
    public static string Find(string directory)
    {
        var files=Directory.GetFiles(directory).Where(p=>string.Equals(Path.GetExtension(p),".png",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).ToArray();
        if(files.Length!=1)throw new IOException("Revit produced "+files.Length+" PNG images for this preview. Files: "+string.Join(", ",Directory.GetFiles(directory).Select(Path.GetFileName))+". Folder: "+directory);
        if(new FileInfo(files[0]).Length==0)throw new IOException("Revit produced an empty preview image: "+files[0]);
        return files[0];
    }
}

===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/RevitGeometryContext.cs =====
using System.Globalization;
using System.Runtime.ExceptionServices;
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;

namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

/// <summary>
/// Thin CustomExporter adapter. Revit callbacks are translated into
/// <see cref="GeometryStream"/> events; all bookkeeping (link and instance
/// stacks, crop selection, instance reuse, batching) lives there and is tested
/// without Revit. Runs only on Revit's API thread; progress is pumped between
/// callbacks, never from another thread.
///
/// No exception is allowed to cross back into Revit's native export loop
/// (Revit may rewrap it and lose the message). A failure or cancel is stored,
/// Revit is asked to stop through <see cref="IsCanceled"/>, and
/// <see cref="ThrowIfStopped"/> rethrows the original after Export returns.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class RevitGeometryContext : IExportContext
{
    private readonly GeometryStream stream;
    private readonly ExportProgressDialog progress;
    private readonly ExportTrace trace;
    private readonly int levelOfDetail;
    private readonly string[] parameters;
    private readonly Dictionary<Document, string> documentIds = new Dictionary<Document, string>();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, (PlainMaterial Material, string Key)> materials = new Dictionary<string, (PlainMaterial, string)>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private string? pendingLinkKey;
    private long elementCallbacks;

    public Exception? Failure { get; private set; }
    public int SymbolIdFailures { get; private set; }
    public int MaterialFailures { get; private set; }
    public ExportStatistics Stats => stream.Stats;

    public RevitGeometryContext(GeometryStream stream, ExportProgressDialog progress, ExportTrace trace, int levelOfDetail, string[] parameters)
    {
        this.stream = stream; this.progress = progress; this.trace = trace; this.levelOfDetail = levelOfDetail; this.parameters = parameters;
    }

    private Document CurrentDocument => (Document)stream.CurrentDocument!;

    private bool Stopped => Failure != null || progress.CancelRequested;

    /// <summary>Call after CustomExporter.Export: rethrows a stored failure, or
    /// reports a cancel that stopped the export early.</summary>
    public void ThrowIfStopped()
    {
        if (Failure != null) ExceptionDispatchInfo.Capture(Failure).Throw();
        if (progress.CancelRequested) throw new OperationCanceledException("Export cancelled");
    }

    private void Fail(Exception ex) { if (Failure == null) { Failure = ex; trace.Line("Stopping the Revit export: " + ex.GetType().Name + ": " + ex.Message); } }

    public bool Start() => !Stopped;
    public void Finish() { }
    public bool IsCanceled() => Stopped;

    public RenderNodeAction OnViewBegin(ViewNode node) { node.LevelOfDetail = levelOfDetail; return RenderNodeAction.Proceed; }
    public void OnViewEnd(ElementId elementId) { }

    public RenderNodeAction OnElementBegin(ElementId elementId)
    {
        if (Stopped) return RenderNodeAction.Skip;
        try { return BeginElement(elementId); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); return RenderNodeAction.Skip; }
    }

    private RenderNodeAction BeginElement(ElementId elementId)
    {
        var doc = CurrentDocument;
        var element = doc.GetElement(elementId);
        bool isLink = element is RevitLinkInstance;
        if (isLink) pendingLinkKey = element!.UniqueId;
        string category = ExportText.Clean(element?.Category?.Name ?? "Unknown", 256);
        string key = stream.LinkChain + "::" + (element?.UniqueId ?? elementId.ToString());
        var info = new ElementInfo
        {
            Key = key,
            // RVT link instances are always entered; their own elements are
            // tested against the crop one by one inside the link.
            IsModel = element?.Category?.CategoryType == CategoryType.Model,
            IsLinkInstance = isLink,
            Category = category,
            Label = category + " | " + ExportText.Clean(element?.Name, 120) + " | id " + elementId + (stream.InLink ? " | linked" : ""),
        };
        if (!isLink && element != null)
        {
            var box = element.get_BoundingBox(null);
            if (box != null)
            {
                var corners = ExportRegion.BoxPoints(box).ToArray();
                info.Min = new Point3(corners.Min(p => p.X), corners.Min(p => p.Y), corners.Min(p => p.Z));
                info.Max = new Point3(corners.Max(p => p.X), corners.Max(p => p.Y), corners.Max(p => p.Z));
            }
        }
        string linkChain = stream.LinkChain;
        info.Identity = surface => Identity(doc, element, key, linkChain, surface);
        bool proceed = stream.BeginElement(info);
        if (++elementCallbacks % 50 == 0) Pump();
        return proceed ? RenderNodeAction.Proceed : RenderNodeAction.Skip;
    }

    public void OnElementEnd(ElementId elementId) { if (Stopped) return; try { stream.EndElement(); } catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); } }

    public RenderNodeAction OnInstanceBegin(InstanceNode node)
    {
        if (Stopped) return RenderNodeAction.Skip;
        try
        {
            string? symbol = null;
            try { symbol = node.GetSymbolGeometryId()?.AsUniqueIdentifier(); }
            catch (Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ApplicationException) { SymbolIdFailures++; }
            return stream.BeginInstance(Adapter.Matrix(node.GetTransform()), symbol) ? RenderNodeAction.Proceed : RenderNodeAction.Skip;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); return RenderNodeAction.Skip; }
    }

    public void OnInstanceEnd(InstanceNode node) { if (Stopped) return; try { stream.EndInstance(); } catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); } }

    public RenderNodeAction OnLinkBegin(LinkNode node)
    {
        if (Stopped) return RenderNodeAction.Skip;
        try
        {
            // Documents are compared by the stream's own push/pop balance, never by
            // reference: Revit can return a new Document wrapper on every call.
            var doc = node.GetDocument();
            string key = pendingLinkKey ?? node.SymbolId.ToString();
            pendingLinkKey = null;
            bool proceed = stream.BeginLink(Adapter.Matrix(node.GetTransform()), doc, key);
            if (proceed) trace.Line("Entering link " + ExportText.Clean(doc?.Title, 200) + " (" + key + ")");
            return proceed ? RenderNodeAction.Proceed : RenderNodeAction.Skip;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); return RenderNodeAction.Skip; }
    }

    public void OnLinkEnd(LinkNode node) { if (Stopped) return; try { stream.EndLink(); } catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); } }

    public RenderNodeAction OnFaceBegin(FaceNode node) => RenderNodeAction.Proceed;
    public void OnFaceEnd(FaceNode node) { }
    public void OnRPC(RPCNode node) { }
    public void OnLight(LightNode node) { }

    public void OnMaterial(MaterialNode node)
    {
        if (Stopped) return;
        try { SetMaterial(node); } catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); }
    }

    private void SetMaterial(MaterialNode node)
    {
        var doc = CurrentDocument;
        var native = node.Color;
        string cacheKey = DocumentId(doc) + "|" + node.MaterialId.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "|" + node.Transparency.ToString("R", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "|" + (native.IsValid ? native.Red + "," + native.Green + "," + native.Blue : "invalid");
        if (!materials.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var cached))
        {
            var material = ReadMaterial(doc, node);
            cached = (material, Contract.Hash(Contract.Encode(material)));
            materials.Add(cacheKey, cached);
        }
        stream.SetMaterial(cached.Material, cached.Key);
    }

    private PlainMaterial ReadMaterial(Document doc, MaterialNode node)
    {
        var source = doc.GetElement(node.MaterialId) as Material;
        var color = source?.Color ?? node.Color;
        if (!color.IsValid) color = node.Color;
        var material = new PlainMaterial
        {
            Name = ExportText.Clean(source?.Name ?? "Default", 256),
            Red = color.IsValid ? color.Red / 255d : .7, Green = color.IsValid ? color.Green / 255d : .7, Blue = color.IsValid ? color.Blue / 255d : .7,
            Opacity = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(1, 1 - node.Transparency)), Roughness = .8
        };
        if (source == null) return material;
        try
        {
            if (doc.GetElement(source.AppearanceAssetId) is AppearanceAssetElement appearance)
            {
                var asset = appearance.GetRenderingAsset();
                if (asset.FindByName("generic_glossiness") is Autodesk.Revit.DB.Visual.AssetPropertyDouble gloss) material.Roughness = Math.Max(.08, Math.Sqrt(1 - Math.Max(0, Math.Min(1, gloss.Value))));
                if (asset.FindByName("generic_is_metal") is Autodesk.Revit.DB.Visual.AssetPropertyBoolean metal && metal.Value) material.Metallic = 1;
            }
            if (doc.GetElement(source.CutForegroundPatternId) is FillPatternElement patternElement)
            {
                var pattern = patternElement.GetFillPattern();
                material.CutPattern = new CutPatternDefinition
                {
                    Name = ExportText.Clean(pattern.Name, 256), Target = pattern.Target.ToString(), Solid = pattern.IsSolidFill,
                    Grids = pattern.GetFillGrids().Select(g => new CutGridDefinition { Angle = g.Angle, OriginXFeet = g.Origin.U, OriginYFeet = g.Origin.V, OffsetFeet = g.Offset, ShiftFeet = g.Shift, SegmentsFeet = g.GetSegments().ToArray() }).ToArray()
                };
            }
        }
        catch (Autodesk.Revit.Exceptions.ApplicationException) { MaterialFailures++; } // appearance details are optional; colour is kept
        return material;
    }

    public void OnPolymesh(PolymeshTopology mesh)
    {
        if (Stopped || !stream.WantsGeometry) return;
        try { AddMesh(mesh); } catch (Exception ex) { Fail(ex); }
    }

    private void AddMesh(PolymeshTopology mesh)
    {
        var nativePoints = mesh.GetPoints();
        var points = new Point3[nativePoints.Count];
        for (int i = 0; i < points.Length; i++) { var p = nativePoints[i]; points[i] = new Point3(p.X, p.Y, p.Z); }
        var facets = mesh.GetFacets();
        var corners = new int[facets.Count * 3];
        for (int i = 0; i < facets.Count; i++) { var f = facets[i]; corners[i * 3] = f.V1; corners[i * 3 + 1] = f.V2; corners[i * 3 + 2] = f.V3; }
        var nativeNormals = mesh.GetNormals();
        var normals = new Point3[nativeNormals.Count];
        for (int i = 0; i < normals.Length; i++) { var n = nativeNormals[i]; normals[i] = new Point3(n.X, n.Y, n.Z); }
        var layout = mesh.DistributionOfNormals == DistributionOfNormals.AtEachPoint ? NormalLayout.PerVertex : mesh.DistributionOfNormals == DistributionOfNormals.OnEachFacet ? NormalLayout.PerFace : NormalLayout.Single;
        stream.AddMesh(points, corners, normals, layout);
        Pump();
    }

    /// <summary>Keeps the window responsive. A cancel is seen by IsCanceled at the next element.</summary>
    private void Pump()
    {
        var s = stream.Stats;
        progress.Pump("Collecting model geometry", new ExportProgressCounts { Elements = s.ElementsBegun, PlacedTriangles = s.FacetsKept + s.TrianglesReused, Reused = s.InstancesReused, Current = stream.CurrentElementLabel });
        trace.Heartbeat(() => "elements " + s.ElementsBegun + " (exported " + s.ElementsExported + ", outside crop " + s.SkippedOutsideCrop + "), facets received " + s.FacetsReceived + ", kept " + s.FacetsKept + ", reused instances " + s.InstancesReused + ", culled " + s.InstancesCulled + ", current: " + (stream.CurrentElementLabel ?? "-"));
    }

    private string DocumentId(Document doc)
    {
        if (!documentIds.TryGetValue(doc, out var id)) { id = ExportText.Clean(doc.ProjectInformation?.UniqueId ?? doc.Title, 256); documentIds.Add(doc, id); }
        return id;
    }

    private ElementIdentity Identity(Document doc, Element? e, string key, string link, PlainMaterial surface)
    {
        var type = e == null ? null : doc.GetElement(e.GetTypeId()) as ElementType;
        var identity = new ElementIdentity
        {
            UniqueId = e?.UniqueId ?? key, DocumentId = DocumentId(doc), LinkInstanceId = string.IsNullOrEmpty(link) ? null : link,
            ElementId = e?.Id.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "", Category = ExportText.Clean(e?.Category?.Name, 256),
            FamilyName = ExportText.Clean(type?.FamilyName, 256), TypeName = ExportText.Clean(type?.Name, 256),
            LevelName = e == null ? "" : ExportText.Clean((doc.GetElement(e.LevelId) as Level)?.Name, 256),
            MaterialName = surface.Name, CutPatternName = surface.CutPattern?.Name ?? ""
        };
        if (e == null) return identity;
        identity.Mark = ExportText.Clean(e.get_Parameter(BuiltInParameter.ALL_MODEL_MARK)?.AsString(), 2048);
        foreach (string name in parameters.Take(20))
        {
            var p = e.LookupParameter(name) ?? type?.LookupParameter(name);
            if (p == null) continue;
            string value = p.StorageType == StorageType.String ? p.AsString() ?? ""
                : p.AsValueString() ?? (p.StorageType == StorageType.Integer ? p.AsInteger().ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
                : p.StorageType == StorageType.ElementId ? p.AsElementId().Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
                : p.StorageType == StorageType.Double ? p.AsDouble().ToString("R", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : "");
            identity.Parameters[ExportText.Clean(name, 256)] = ExportText.Clean(value, 2048);
        }
        return identity;
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/RibbonApplication.cs =====
using Autodesk.Revit.DB;
using Autodesk.Revit.UI;
using System.Reflection;
namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;
public sealed class RibbonApplication:IExternalApplication
{
    public Result OnStartup(UIControlledApplication app){
        var panel=app.CreateRibbonPanel("Offline 3D PDF v"+ExportCommand.ExporterVersion);
        var button=(PushButton)panel.AddItem(new PushButtonData("Offline3D.Export","Export 3D PDF",Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location,typeof(ExportCommand).FullName));
        button.ToolTip="Export the open plan, RCP, section, elevation or orthographic 3D view (or the model views on the open sheet) as a PDF with clickable offline 3D, cropped to the view. Automatic registration is checked before delivery.";
        button.AvailabilityClassName=typeof(ExportAvailability).FullName;
        return Result.Succeeded;
    }
    public Result OnShutdown(UIControlledApplication app)=>Result.Succeeded;
}
public sealed class ExportAvailability:IExternalCommandAvailability
{
    public bool IsCommandAvailable(UIApplication app,CategorySet categories){
        var ui=app.ActiveUIDocument;
        return ui!=null&&!ui.Document.IsFamilyDocument&&(ui.ActiveView is ViewSheet||ExportTarget.IsSupportedView(ui.ActiveView));
    }
}


===== FILE: src/RevitExporter/SheetBounds.cs =====
namespace Offline3D.RevitExporter;

// Plain geometry, shared with the portable regression tests. Bounds include
// viewport bodies and view-title outlines; touching edges are not ambiguous.
internal readonly struct SheetBounds
{
    private readonly double left,bottom,right,top;
    public SheetBounds(double left,double bottom,double right,double top)
    {
        if(double.IsNaN(left)||double.IsNaN(bottom)||double.IsNaN(right)||double.IsNaN(top)||double.IsInfinity(left)||double.IsInfinity(bottom)||double.IsInfinity(right)||double.IsInfinity(top)||right<left||top<bottom)
            throw new System.IO.InvalidDataException("Invalid viewport bounds.");
        this.left=left;this.bottom=bottom;this.right=right;this.top=top;
    }
    public bool Overlaps(SheetBounds other)=>left<other.right&&right>other.left&&bottom<other.top&&top>other.bottom;
}
